Ablation confirms every component is necessary, and replacing adaptive weighting with concatenation causes recall collapse at 6 hours (F1: 0.14). These results establish regime-conditioned adaptive fusion as a necessary design principle for multi-modal financial forecasting.

</details>


### [70] [Directional Influence Function: Estimating Training Data Influence in Constrained Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23388)
*Xin Wang,R. Tyrrell Rockafellar,Xuegang,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统影响函数（IF）在约束学习场景下因忽略可行域变化而失效的问题，提出方向影响函数（DIF），通过将约束学习的最优性条件建模为变分不等式（VI），分析数据扰动对VI的影响，从而在保持可行性的前提下准确估计样本贡献。实验表明，DIF在约束线性回归和公平性约束CNN上均能精准恢复留一法重训练结果，显著优于IF和罚函数IF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束学习（如公平性、安全性、鲁棒性约束）日益普遍，但经典影响函数（IF）在约束设置下不可靠——数据扰动会同时改变目标函数和可行域，导致估计结果违反可行性约束，无法准确衡量样本对模型解的影响。

Method: 将约束学习的最优性条件形式化为变分不等式（VI），在此基础上提出方向影响函数（DIF），通过分析训练数据扰动如何影响VI的解来估计样本贡献，显式地将约束纳入影响估计框架。

Result: 在约束线性回归中，DIF能准确恢复留一法重训练结果，而IF和基于罚函数的IF存在显著偏差；在公平性约束CNN上，DIF能精确预测数据移除后的测试损失变化，与实际重训练结果高度一致。

Conclusion: DIF为约束学习提供了一种高效、可靠的数据归因工具，解决了传统IF在约束场景下的局限性。

Abstract: As constrained learning becomes increasingly common, models are trained under explicit feasibility requirements to enforce fairness, safety, robustness, regulariza- tion, and physics or logic constraints. Understanding how training samples in- fluence the model solution (e.g., learned parameters) is crucial for interpretability and robustness. The classical influence function (IF) estimates sample contribu- tions via local sensitivity analysis, measuring how the solution changes when a specific training sample is perturbed or removed. However, IF becomes unreli- able in constrained settings: data perturbations can reshape both the objective and the feasible region, leading to estimates that violate feasibility. In response, we propose the Directional Influence Function (DIF), a novel estimator that explicitly incorporates these constraints into influence estimation. DIF formulates the opti- mality conditions of constrained learning as a variational inequality (VI) and ana- lyzes how perturbing training data affects this VI. We validate DIF on constrained linear regression and demonstrate that it recovers leave-one-out retraining results, whereas IF and penalty-based IF exhibit significant bias. We further apply DIF to fairness-constrained CNNs, where DIF accurately predicts test loss changes under data removal and aligns closely with actual retraining. Our results establish DIF as an efficient and reliable tool for data attribution in constrained learning.

</details>


### [71] [When Can Depth Replace Precision? A Resource Theory of Quantized Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23390)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定输入-输出映射下，低比特残差计算能否替代缺失的数值精度。通过将量化残差系统建模为从低比特操作库中选择字段的纯调度问题，并利用松弛控制刻画其无限深度极限。发现目标与松弛可达集的距离是精确结构下限，深度增加无法消除。纯调度以O(D⁻¹)或O(D⁻ᵛᵉʳᵗʰᵉᵗᵃ+D⁻¹)速率逼近松弛类。执行语义可逆转结论：全状态写回引入Dρz惩罚并冻结残差更新，而增量误差反馈将增长替换为有界进位项并满足精确共格守恒律。固定教师逆问题使速率紧确：深度L的一阶高精度比较器需D=Θ(L)。学习码本增加元数据资源，状态相关路由引入混合事件条件。验证原对偶边界可在训练前给出可行、不可能或未决决策。配套软件和Lean 4验证了核心离散逻辑。深度替代精度仅相对于声明的库、视界、执行语义和路由模型成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在低比特量化残差系统中，增加深度能否补偿数值精度损失这一核心问题，为低精度高效计算提供理论边界。

Method: 将量化残差系统形式化为有限视界上的纯调度问题，引入松弛控制分析无限深度极限，利用有界变差和Hölder依赖条件推导收敛速率，区分全状态写回与增量误差反馈两种执行语义，结合固定教师逆问题、学习码本和状态路由等扩展。

Result: 证明了目标与松弛可达集的距离是精确结构下限；纯调度逼近速率为O(D⁻¹)或O(D⁻ᵛᵉʳᵗʰᵉᵗᵃ+D⁻¹)；全状态写回导致线性惩罚，增量误差反馈仅为有界进位；固定教师下精度匹配需D=Θ(L)。

Conclusion: 深度替代精度是条件性的，取决于操作库、视界、执行语义和路由模型。验证了可行/不可能/未决的三类决策边界，提供了可验证的分析框架。

Abstract: When can additional low-bit residual computation replace missing numerical precision for a fixed input-output map? We model a quantized residual system over a fixed horizon as a pure schedule selecting fields from a declared low-bit operation library, and use relaxed controls to characterize its infinite-depth limit. The distance from the target to the closed relaxed reachable set is the exact structural floor: no increase in depth can remove it for that library. Pure schedules approach the relaxed class at rate $O(D^{-1})$ under bounded-variation time dependence and $O(D^{-\vartheta}+D^{-1})$ under Holder dependence of exponent $\vartheta$. Execution arithmetic can reverse this conclusion: full-state write-back introduces a $Dρ_z$ penalty and can freeze residual updates, whereas increment error feedback replaces this growth by a bounded carry term and obeys an exact common-lattice conservation law. A fixed-teacher converse makes this rate sharp: for coherent depth-$L$ first-order high-precision comparators, accuracy matching requires $D=Θ(L)$. Learned codebooks add a metadata resource, while state-dependent routing introduces hybrid event conditions. Verified primal and dual bounds yield feasible, impossible, or unresolved decisions before training. Companion software implements the workflow, and Lean 4 machine-checks the exact discrete core. Depth replaces precision only relative to a declared library, horizon, execution semantics, and routing model.

</details>


### [72] [A Statistical Difference between Single-Layer Learning and Hierarchical Learning in Wide Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23397)
*Sumio Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究三层宽神经网络，证明训练输入到隐藏层的权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差，且固定权重会导致参数空间奇异性，而训练权重则不会，表明奇异性在宽神经网络中也很重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解层级神经网络在宽极限下的数学性质，特别是对比两种理论框架（固定核与优化核）对泛化性能的影响。

Method: 研究有限但大量隐藏单元的三层神经网络，比较训练输入到隐藏层权重与固定该权重两种设置下的泛化误差和参数空间奇异性。

Result: 训练输入到隐藏层权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差；固定权重设置中存在参数空间奇异性，而训练权重的设置中没有。

Conclusion: 奇异性在宽神经网络中仍然扮演重要角色，支持参数可以远离初始化的理论框架优于固定核框架。

Abstract: Hierarchical neural networks are widely used in artificial intelligence, yet their mathematical properties remain incompletely understood. In the infinite-width limit, two different theoretical frameworks have been proposed. One reduces deep learning to kernel regression with a fixed kernel by assuming that the parameters remain close to their initialization, whereas the other allows the parameters to move away from their initialization, requiring the kernel itself to be optimized.
  In this paper, we study a three-layer neural network with a finite but large number of hidden units. We show that training the input-to-hidden weights yields a smaller generalization error than keeping them fixed. Furthermore, the latter setting exhibits singularities in the parameter space, whereas the former does not. These findings indicate that singularities play an essential role even in wide neural networks.

</details>


### [73] [Transfer Learning Architectures for Scalable Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23404)
*Jaewook Lee,Ethan Errington,Christian D. Lorenz,Miao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地将迁移学习（Transfer Learning）作为多保真贝叶斯优化的代理模型引擎，在分子与材料科学任务中对比了11种迁移学习代理与4种高斯过程方法。结果表明，高斯过程在平滑低维函数上表现优异，但在分子与材料问题上性能最差；迁移学习代理能以更少计算量获得更优解，且贪婪利用迁移学习均值比不确定性驱动的探索更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程作为多保真贝叶斯优化的默认代理模型存在两大缺陷：数据累积时扩展性差，且假设搜索空间平滑，而这在分子与材料搜索空间中常被违反。迁移学习虽已用于属性预测，但尚未作为闭环优化引擎被系统测试。

Method: 在相同选择规则、保真度预算和模型规模下，对11种迁移学习代理与4种高斯过程方法进行基准测试，涵盖合成函数、真实化学与材料问题等9项任务。采集策略在不同代理间保持固定，以隔离代理模型自身的贡献。

Result: 高斯过程在平滑低维函数上获胜，但在分子与材料问题上表现最差；迁移学习代理以更少计算量达到更优解。不确定性驱动的探索并不总是有益，校准度不能预测优化性能，贪婪利用迁移学习均值更为稳健。

Conclusion: 迁移学习是分子与材料多保真贝叶斯优化的首选代理模型，其优势源于代理模型本身而非采集策略。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MFBO) to balance cheap, approximate evaluations against scarce, expensive ones, with a predictive surrogate at its core. Gaussian processes (GPs) are the default choice, but they scale poorly as data accumulate and assume a smooth landscape that molecular and materials search spaces routinely violate. Transfer learning offers an alternative suited to this regime: it learns a representation from abundant cheap data and adapts it to sparse expensive data. Despite its use in property prediction, transfer learning has not been tested as the engine of a closed-loop optimization. Here we benchmark eleven transfer-learning surrogates against four GP methods under an identical selection rule, fidelity budget, and model size, across nine tasks spanning synthetic functions to real chemistry and materials problems. GPs win on smooth, low-dimensional functions but perform worst on molecular and materials problems, where transfer-learning surrogates reach substantially better solutions using far less computation. Because acquisition policy is held fixed across surrogates, this advantage is attributable to the surrogate itself. Uncertainty-driven exploration is not reliably beneficial, and calibration does not predict optimization performance, so greedy exploitation of the transfer-learned mean is the more robust default. Transfer learning is therefore the surrogate of choice for molecular and materials MFBO.

</details>


### [74] [Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG: A Comparative Study of Direct and ECG-Mediated Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23406)
*Bo Wu,Haoling Wang,Zhuodiao Kuang,Kateryna Shapovalenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模生理相关性分析发现，PPG与动脉血压的耦合强度（|r|=0.247, p<0.001）显著高于ECG（r=0.018, p=0.187），挑战了ECG是更优中间表征的传统假设。基于这一发现，系统比较了直接PPG-to-BP预测与ECG中介管线，在174万段数据上，直接PPG预测达到BHS Grade A（SBP MAE=4.82 mmHg, DBP MAE=4.31 mmHg），优于所有ECG中介方法（仅Grade B）。结果表明可直接从可穿戴PPG信号实现准确连续血压监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究假设ECG与BP有更强生理关联，常通过从PPG重构ECG再间接估计BP。然而ECG在可穿戴场景中获取不便且增加复杂性。本文旨在验证PPG是否能直接用于连续无袖带血压监测，挑战ECG必需性的传统假设。

Method: 1) 在MIMIC-III波形数据库上进行大规模生理相关性分析，比较PPG-ABP和ECG-ABP的相关性；2) 系统比较直接PPG-to-BP预测与ECG中介管线，使用多种最新深度学习模型；3) 在174万段数据（3,127名患者）上进行评估。

Result: PPG与ABP的相关性（|r|=0.247, p<0.001）显著强于ECG与ABP（r=0.018, p=0.187）。直接PPG-to-BP预测达到BHS Grade A（SBP MAE=4.82 mmHg, DBP MAE=4.31 mmHg），优于所有ECG中介方法（仅Grade B）。

Conclusion: 准确的连续血压监测可直接从可穿戴PPG信号实现，无需ECG中介，从而为实际连接健康系统构建更简单、高效的管线。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring is essential for connected health systems and wearable devices, enabling early detection, longitudinal tracking, and personalized management of cardiovascular disease. Many prior approaches attempt to estimate BP indirectly by reconstructing electrocardiography (ECG) from photoplethysmography (PPG), assuming ECG provides a stronger physiological link to BP. However, ECG sensing is less accessible in wearable settings and may introduce unnecessary complexity.
  In this work, we first perform a large-scale physiological correlation analysis on the MIMIC-III waveform database, revealing that PPG exhibits substantially stronger coupling with arterial blood pressure (ABP) ($|r|=0.247$, $p<0.001$) than ECG does ($r=0.018$, $p=0.187$), challenging the assumption that ECG provides a superior intermediate representation. Motivated by this insight, we conduct a systematic comparison between direct PPG-to-BP prediction and ECG-mediated pipelines using multiple state-of-the-art deep learning models.
  Across 1.74M segments from 3,127 patients, direct PPG-to-BP prediction achieves British Hypertension Society Grade A performance ($\mathrm{MAE}_{\mathrm{SBP}} = 4.82 mmHg$, $\mathrm{MAE}_{\mathrm{DBP}} = 4.31 mmHg$), outperforming all ECG-mediated approaches, which achieve only Grade B accuracy.
  Our findings suggest that accurate continuous BP monitoring can be achieved directly from wearable PPG signals, enabling simpler, more efficient pipelines for real-world connected health systems.

</details>


### [75] [Harmonized Interpretable ECG Waveform Features for Robust Cross-Dataset Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23412)
*Jie Lin,Weijie Sun,Sunil V. Kalmady,Anita Khalafbeigi,Abram Hindle,Padma Kaul,Russell Greiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于原始心电波形的统一特征表示（FeatureDB），在跨医院数据集的心衰分类、30天全因死亡率和窦性心律ECG的30天死亡率三项任务上，XGBoost模型达到内部AUROC 0.79-0.82、跨数据集AUROC 0.74-0.78，外部AUROC保留源站点内部性能的至少90%，表明该透明、可解释的特征接口在跨站点临床预测中具有稳健的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图（ECG）心血管风险预测模型因协议、人群和测量差异难以跨医院迁移，亟需一种能缓解厂商特异性测量不匹配、支持可解释和跨数据集泛化的统一特征表示方法。

Method: 从原始心电波形直接计算统一可解释特征表示（FeatureDB），包括形态学/心率变异性总结（morphology/HRV summaries）和紧凑时频描述子（自回归与小波特征），在MIMIC-IV和Alberta Cohort两个大规模队列上训练XGBoost模型，进行患者分离的内部测试和双向外部测试。

Result: 内部AUROC为0.79-0.82，跨数据集AUROC为0.74-0.78；外部AUROC保留源站点内部性能至少90%；统一特征集的内部AUROC在数据集原生机器测量模型的10%以内。端到端ConvNeXt模型内部AUROC更高，但统一特征表示在跨数据集迁移稳定性方面具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 一致的波形衍生特征接口能够保持模型性能，支持真实场景的外部验证，并为跨站点临床预测提供了一种透明且可替代的方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used for cardiovascular risk prediction, yet models often fail to transfer across hospitals because of protocol, population, and measurement differences. We benchmark cross-dataset generalization on three tasks - heart failure classification, 30-day all-cause mortality, and 30-day mortality among sinus-rhythm ECGs - using two large cohorts (MIMIC-IV and the Alberta Cohort). To reduce vendor-specific measurement mismatch, we build a harmonized, interpretable feature representation computed directly from raw waveforms: FeatureDB morphology/heart-rate-variability summaries plus compact time-frequency descriptors (autoregressive and wavelet features). We train XGBoost models on this unified feature space and evaluate with patient-disjoint internal and bidirectional external testing. We pre-specify two hypotheses: (H1) external AUROC retains at least 90% of source-site internal AUROC under transfer, and (H2) internal AUROC of the harmonized feature set stays within 10% of dataset-native machine-measurement models. Across tasks, internal AUROC is 0.79-0.82 and cross-dataset AUROC is 0.74-0.78, with larger and direction-dependent AUPRC shifts under transfer. As an exploratory benchmark, an end-to-end ConvNeXt model trained directly on raw ECG waveforms with age and sex achieves higher internal AUROC, while the harmonized representation remains competitive in relative cross-dataset transfer stability. These findings show that a consistent waveform-derived feature interface preserves performance, supports realistic external validation, and provides a transparent alternative for cross-site clinical prediction.

</details>


### [76] [Short-Term Pain for Long-Term Gain: Adaptive Experiment with Post-Commitment Reward Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23432)
*Puping Jiang,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对学习环境中的决策者在短期最优与长期收益之间的权衡问题，提出RAEC算法（适用于离散选项）和ROSCOC算法（适用于连续凸优化/投资组合选择），在实验阶段预留部分轮次识别承诺后的最优选项，其余轮次最小化短期遗憾，并给出了各参数区间的遗憾上界与匹配的极小化下界，还通过实验验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决决策者在自适应实验中面临的困境：短期最优行动可能不利于长期收益最大化。需要理解在实验阶段（可自适应测试多个选项）和承诺阶段（必须承诺单一选项，且承诺后奖励可能发生变化）之间的根本权衡。

Method: 提出RAEC算法，在实验阶段预留固定比例轮次用于识别承诺后最优选项，其余轮次最小化短期遗憾；建立各参数区间的遗憾上界并推导匹配的极小化下界。扩展研究中，利用先验结构知识（奖励变化前后的关系）识别排序变化的关键成分；针对凹承诺奖励和投资组合选择问题，提出ROSCOC算法，将预留探索历史直接转化为承诺组合。

Result: RAEC算法在各参数区间均有紧的遗憾上界，且与极小化下界匹配；ROSCOC算法在承诺奖励为凹函数及投资组合场景下同样达到紧的遗憾界；数值实验证实两种算法均达到理论预测的遗憾值，并优于基线算法。

Conclusion: 本文为自适应实验与承诺后奖励偏移问题提供了理论最优的算法框架，揭示了短期表现与长期承诺之间权衡的精确代价，并为存在先验结构知识或连续决策空间的情形提供了有效扩展方案。

Abstract: Decision-makers in learning environments face a dilemma when their short-term optimal actions may not favor their long-term benefits the most. To understand the fundamental tradeoff behind the dilemma, we study adaptive experimentation with post-commitment reward shifts. During an experiment phase, the decision-maker may adaptively test multiple options; during a subsequent commitment phase, the decision-maker must commit to a single option, whose reward may differ from its pre-commitment reward. We propose the Reserved Arm Eliminations for Commitment (RAEC) algorithm, which reserves a predetermined portion of the experiment phase to identify the best post-shift option while using the remaining rounds to minimize short-run regret. We establish regret upper bounds for RAEC across all parameter regimes and matching minimax lower bounds, providing a tight characterization of the cost of balancing short-term performance and long-term commitment.
  We also study two extensions. With prior structural knowledge linking pre- and post-shift rewards, we show that correctly identifying the ranking-changing component of the shift is more important than estimating its absolute magnitude. For settings with concave commitment rewards and portfolio choice, we develop the Reserved Online Stochastic Convex Optimization for Commitment (ROSCOC) algorithm, which directly converts its reserved exploration history into a commitment portfolio and achieves tight regret bound. Finally, we also conduct numerical experiments which confirm that our proposed algorithms achieve the desired regret predicted by our theory, and also outperform other baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [77] [PerturbPFN: Probing the Limits of Synthetic Priors in Drug Perturbation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23447)
*Yuche Gao,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Siyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerturbPFN是一种基于PFN的摊销模型，通过层次化合成结构先验预测未知靶点的化学扰动响应。模型不直接回归高维表达，而是推断潜在系统图、稀疏原子干预靶点及强度，并通过SCM解码器传播效应。模型完全在合成数据上训练，无需测试时梯度更新。在单细胞扰动数据和合成基准上，PerturbPFN在扰动预测、靶点识别和调控结构发现方面实现了具有竞争力的性能与低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未知化学扰动的细胞响应极具挑战性，原因包括：靶点和机制未知、表达响应高维、小分子设计空间的实验覆盖有限。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出PerturbPFN模型：(1) 采用PFN风格的结构化上下文学习，完全在合成数据上训练；(2) 不直接回归高维表达，而是推断潜在系统图、稀疏原子干预靶点和干预强度；(3) 通过SCM解码器传播干预效应；(4) 使用生物启发的图与表达模拟器生成先验预测合成训练片段。

Result: 在真实单细胞扰动数据和合成基准上，PerturbPFN在扰动预测、靶点识别和调控结构发现方面取得有竞争力的结果，推理成本低，且能提供靶点、强度和系统结构的可解释中间估计。

Conclusion: PerturbPFN为未知扰动预测提供了与专业基线互补的权衡方案，在保持低推理开销的同时实现了具有竞争力的预测性能，并具备可解释性优势。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to unseen chemical perturbations is challenging due to unknown targets and mechanisms, high-dimensional expression responses, and limited experimental coverage of the large small-molecule design space. We propose PerturbPFN, a PFN-style amortized model for unknown-target perturbation prediction under a hierarchical synthetic structural prior. Instead of directly regressing high-dimensional expression responses, PerturbPFN infers a latent system graph, sparse atomic intervention targets, and intervention strengths, then propagates their effects through an SCM decoder. The model is trained entirely on prior-predictive synthetic episodes generated from biologically motivated graph and expression simulators, enabling structured in-context learning without test-time gradient updates. We evaluate PerturbPFN on both real single-cell perturbation data and synthetic benchmarks, covering effect prediction, target identification, and regulatory structure discovery. Our results show that PerturbPFN offers a complementary trade-off to specialized baselines, achieving competitive perturbation prediction with low inference cost while exposing interpretable intermediate estimates of targets, strengths, and system structure.

</details>


### [78] [Local Regularization Does Not Characterize Multiclass PAC Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23449)
*Eric Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明局部正则化（local regularization）无法刻画多类PAC可学习性，通过构造一个Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数至多为2、真实PAC样本复杂度为O((1/ε)log(1/δ))的可数类，该类的假设为完全图的边、实例为锦标赛，不存在任何局部正则化器能够学习它。核心障碍是循环三角形导致的反转迫使幸存竞争者产生恒定的人口误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Asilis等人曾提出疑问：局部正则化是否刻画了多类PAC可学习性？本文旨在否定这一猜想，揭示局部正则化原则的局限性。

Method: 构造一个反例类：假设为完全图的边，实例为锦标赛。在测试锦标赛上，分数固定边的排序，而训练样本独立移除竞争者。利用循环三角形迫使产生足够的反转，使得幸存竞争者即使在任意大样本量下仍产生恒定的人口误差。

Result: 存在一个可数类，其Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数≤2，真实PAC样本复杂度为O((1/ε)log(1/δ))，但没有任何局部正则化器能够学习它。

Conclusion: 局部正则化不能刻画多类PAC可学习性，多类学习的表征需要更复杂的原则。

Abstract: Local regularization assigns each hypothesis a test-point-dependent score and predicts with a minimum-score hypothesis consistent with the sample. Asilis et al. asked whether this principle characterizes multiclass PAC learnability. We give a negative answer. There is a countable class of Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz dimension at most two with realizable PAC sample complexity \[ O\!\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log\frac{1}δ\right), \] that no local regularizer learns. Hypotheses are edges of complete graphs and instances are tournaments. At a test tournament, the scores fix an edge ranking while the training sample independently removes competitors. Cyclic triangles force enough inversions that surviving competitors produce constant population error at arbitrarily large sample sizes.

</details>


### [79] [Generalization bounds and sample complexity for remaining useful life prediction from complete degradation trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23454)
*Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了剩余寿命预测的样本复杂度理论框架，包含七个主要结果：推导了分布无关的泛化界（误差率O(B²√(p/n))）和极小下界（最优率Θ(p/n)）；证明物理知识可将数据需求降低两个量级，并给出了伯恩斯坦型最优分析和闭式惩罚项；刻画了机队变异性导致的偏差-方差权衡和右删失数据的效率损失。在涡扇、电池和轴承基准上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前剩余寿命预测缺乏理论指导，实践者不知道针对给定模型和精度目标需要多少失效数据样本，本文旨在建立样本复杂度框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用统计学习理论方法，推导了分布无关泛化界、极小极大下界、伯恩斯坦型分析等理论结果，并通过跨领域基准验证。

Result: 建立了七个主要理论结果，包括学习率、物理知识加速效果和数据质量影响的理论刻画，并在涡扇、电池和轴承基准上验证了预测精度在2-3倍因子范围内。

Conclusion: 为数据收集规划、模型复杂度选择和物理模型假设评估提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Data-driven remaining useful life (RUL) prediction requires complete degradation trajectories for training, yet such run-to-failure data are scarce and expensive. Practitioners currently lack principled guidance on how many failure examples suffice for a given model and accuracy target. This paper develops a sample complexity framework for RUL prediction comprising seven main results organised around three themes. First, we establish fundamental learning rates: a distribution-free generalization bound shows that the uniform deviation of the mean squared error decreases as $O(B^{2}\sqrt{p/n})$, where $p$ is the model complexity and $n$ the number of trajectories, and a minimax lower bound proves that the $Θ(p/n)$ rate is unimprovable.} \rev{Second, we quantify how domain knowledge accelerates learning: incorporating degradation physics reduces data requirements by up to two orders of magnitude for deep networks, a Bernstein-type analysis achieves the minimax-optimal $O(p/n)$ rate under high signal-to-noise conditions, and closed-form penalties reveal when an incorrectly assumed physics model hurts rather than helps. Third, we characterise the impact of data quality: fleet variability induces an irreducible bias$-$variance tradeoff, while right-censored observations suffer an efficiency loss that depends critically on the degradation class.} Closed-form expressions are provided for exponential, power-law, and stretched-exponential degradation. \rev{Cross-domain validation against published turbofan, battery, and bearing benchmarks confirms the theoretical predictions within a factor of 2$-$3 on average. The results yield practical guidelines for planning data collection, selecting model complexity, and evaluating physics model assumptions in prognostics applications.

</details>


### [80] [Extending Fourier Neural Operators for Modeling Parameterized and Coupled PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23466)
*Cheng Jing,Uvini Balasuriya Mudiyanselage,Abhishek Verma,Kallol Bera,Shahid Rauf,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化和耦合偏微分方程（PDE）问题，对傅里叶神经算子（FNO）进行了最小化架构修改：一方面通过超网络调制使算子适应物理参数，另一方面系统探索了架构设计以平衡共享结构与跨变量交互，在电容耦合等离子体方程和Gray-Scott系统上实现了55-72%的误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化与耦合PDE是科学与工程建模的核心，但能够同时处理这两方面的神经算子方法仍然有限。

Method: 1) 针对参数化动力学，提出基于超网络的调制机制，将算子条件化为物理参数的函数；2) 针对耦合系统，系统性地探索架构设计选择，调整算子组件以平衡共享结构与跨变量交互，同时保持标准FNO的计算效率。

Result: 在一维电容耦合等离子体方程和Gray-Scott系统等基准PDE上的评估表明，所提方法相比强基线方法实现了55-72%的更低误差。

Conclusion: 有原则的调制机制和系统性的设计探索能够有效提升神经算子处理参数化与耦合PDE问题的性能。

Abstract: Parameterized and coupled partial differential equations (PDEs) are central to modeling phenomena in science and engineering, yet neural operator methods that address both aspects remain limited. We extend Fourier neural operators (FNOs) with minimal architectural modifications along two directions. For parameterized dynamics, we propose a hypernetwork-based modulation that conditions the operator on physical parameters. For coupled systems, we conduct a systematic exploration of architectural choices, examining how operator components can be adapted to balance shared structure with cross-variable interactions while retaining the efficiency of standard FNOs. Evaluations on benchmark PDEs, including the one-dimensional capacitively coupled plasma equations and the Gray-Scott system, show that our methods achieve up to 55-72% lower errors than strong baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of principled modulation and systematic design exploration.

</details>


### [81] [Learning to Optimize: Joint Routing and Flow Allocation on Sparse Non-Euclidean Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23467)
*Haomiao Sun,Fang He,Congyuan Ji,Xindi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道图注意力（DCGA）强化学习框架，联合优化稀疏非欧网络上的环型路径规划、货物流量分配与跨周期服务，在LinerLib基准上实现秒级推理，在大规模问题上取得最优解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏非欧网络上的取送货问题中，环型路径、流量分配与跨周期服务三者紧密耦合，形成离散-连续混合决策空间且可行域高度受限，现有方法难以高效求解。

Method: 提出DCGA框架：1）将网络可达性与需求-服务逻辑分离为两个独立图通道；2）设计基于仿真耦合的约束感知解码器构建合法路径；3）采用端到端强化学习训练。

Result: 在LinerLib基准上，DCGA实现秒级推理，在特定规模以上实例中达到最优解质量，且优势随问题规模增大而扩大；通过稳定性和消融分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 这种结构感知的学习方法为现实路径-流量联合优化提供了高效、低延迟的求解引擎。

Abstract: We study an integrated pickup-and-delivery problem on sparse, non-Euclidean networks that jointly optimizes cyclic routing, cargo flow allocation, and cross-cycle service. The tight coupling of these operational constraints creates a complex discrete-continuous decision space with highly restricted feasible regions. To overcome these computational challenges, we propose Double-Channel Graph Attention (DCGA), an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework. DCGA isolates network reachability and demand-service logic into separate graph channels and constructs valid routes using a simulator-coupled, constraint-informed decoder. Experiments on LinerLib benchmarks demonstrate that DCGA achieves seconds-level inference and delivers state-of-the-art solution quality on instances beyond a specific scale, with its advantage over existing baselines widening significantly as problem size increases. Supported by extensive stability and ablation analyses, our results demonstrate that this structure-aware learning approach provides an effective, low-latency engine for realistic routing-and-flow optimization.

</details>


### [82] [Sparse Gaussian-Mixture-Model Q-Functions via Hadamard Overparametrization for Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23474)
*Minh Vu,Konstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏高斯混合模型Q函数（S-GMM-QFs）的在线离策略策略迭代框架，通过Hadamard过参数化和黎曼优化实现可解释的稀疏化，在参数效率、泛化能力和可解释性上超越或匹配深度RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度RL方法参数冗余、可解释性差，且离策略学习中的分布不匹配问题缺乏有效的几何处理手段。本文旨在通过稀疏化GMM与黎曼几何相结合，实现高效、可解释的在线RL框架。

Method: 采用Hadamard过参数化构建稀疏GMM-Q函数，通过平滑正则化实现可解释的稀疏化；利用经验回放处理分布不匹配；在笛卡尔积黎曼流形上进行在线梯度下降优化，每个高斯分量的均值和协方差显式编码其几何角色。

Result: 数值实验表明，S-GMM-QFs在参数显著减少的情况下匹配或超越深度RL方法，每回合提升速度更快；在低参数场景下，稀疏化深度RL方法性能下降，而S-GMM-QFs因参数效率和可解释性保持强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将黎曼几何与稀疏化过参数化结合，能够在保持模型紧凑和可解释的同时实现高效在线RL，尤其适用于低参数场景下的泛化需求。

Abstract: This paper develops an online, off-policy policy-iteration framework for reinforcement learning (RL), based on sparse Gaussian-mixture-model Q-functions (S-GMM-QFs). The framework reconciles streaming, non-stationary data with the Riemannian structure of the parameter space while handling distributional mismatch through experience replay. S-GMM-QFs are introduced via Hadamard overparametrization, enabling interpretable sparsification through smooth regularization that facilitates Riemannian-based optimization. Overparametrization allows the framework to adaptively identify meaningful components from a large initial pool, yielding sparse models where interpretability emerges naturally from geometry: each component's parameters (means and covariances) explicitly encode its geometric role in the ambient state-action space. These geometric roles are learned through online gradient descent on a smooth objective over a (Cartesian-product) Riemannian manifold. Numerical tests demonstrate that S-GMM-QFs match or exceed deep RL methods while using substantially fewer parameters and achieving faster improvement per observed transition. Notably, parameter efficiency and interpretability combine to maintain strong generalization in low-parameter regimes where sparsified deep RL approaches degrade.

</details>


### [83] [A Multi-stage Constrained Optimization Framework for Data-driven Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23480)
*Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶段约束优化框架（MCOF），通过熵约束VAE、均匀变换和约束优先过滤器方法，解决VAE在约束优化中的三大挑战，在合成问题和ZINC250k药物设计中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE在约束优化中存在三个挑战：潜在空间有效采样、识别对目标和约束有影响的活动决策变量、在不破坏训练稳定性的前提下强制执行约束。

Method: 提出MCOF框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 熵约束VAE（EC-VAE）结合特征选择器，将目标和约束信息嵌入指定潜在变量子集，在低维子空间优化；(2) 均匀变换（UT）模块，通过逐维概率积分变换将不规则聚合后验转化为有界箱上的均匀分布；(3) 约束优先过滤器方法（CPFM），通过交替违反减少和目标减少步骤求解替代问题，无需乘子估计。

Result: 在合成问题上消融每个阶段并恢复了分析最优解；在ZINC250k药物设计任务中，生成的分子满足施加的约束且完全新颖。

Conclusion: MCOF有效解决了VAE约束优化的三大挑战，能够生成可行且多样化的解。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) transform high-dimensional, often noisy data into a compact latent representation, making downstream optimization more tractable. Three challenges persist in VAE-based constrained optimization: (i) sampling effectively within the latent space, (ii) identifying the active decision variables that actually influence the objective and constraints, and (iii) enforcing constraints without destabilizing training. We propose a Multi-stage Constrained Optimization Framework (MCOF). First, an entropy-constrained VAE (EC-VAE) coupled with a feature selector embeds objective and constraint information into a designated subset of latent variables, so that optimization proceeds over a low-dimensional subspace while the remaining coordinates supply solution diversity. Second, a Uniform Transformation (UT) module applies a per-dimension probability integral transform, replacing the irregular aggregate posterior with a uniform distribution over a bounded box and mitigating posterior collapse and Gaussian mixture bias. Third, a constraint-priority filter method (CPFM) solves the resulting surrogate problem by alternating violation-reduction and objective-reduction steps under a filter acceptance test, returning solutions that are feasible for the learned surrogate to a specified tolerance without requiring multiplier estimation. Finally, unselected latent coordinates are resampled to generate diverse decodings of a single optimized solution. We validate MCOF on a synthetic problem, where we ablate each stage and recover the analytic optimum, and on a ZINC250k drug design task, where the generated molecules satisfy the imposed constraints and are entirely novel relative to the training set.

</details>


### [84] [Charging Phase Health Indicators for Battery State-of-Health Estimation: A Systematic Comparison of CC, CV, and Combined Approaches under Cross-Battery Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23482)
*Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了恒流（CC）和恒压（CV）充电阶段的健康指标在电池健康状态估计中的有效性，采用留一电池法（LOBO）验证，发现CC+CV组合指标效果最佳（R²=0.874），且标准5折交叉验证与LOBO验证之间存在119%的性能差距，表明传统评估方法高估了实际精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究从CC和CV充电阶段提取健康指标用于电池SOH估计，但这些指标在跨电池交叉验证条件下的有效性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在NASA电池老化数据集上，采用严格的留一电池法（LOBO）验证，系统比较了仅CC指标、仅CV指标以及CC+CV组合指标集的性能。评估了四个CV阶段指标和CC阶段持续时间，分别进行单独和组合评估。

Result: CC+CV组合方法性能最佳（R²=0.874），表明CC和CV阶段捕获了互补的退化信息。标准5折交叉验证与LOBO验证之间存在119%的性能差距。

Conclusion: CC和CV阶段提供的退化信息具有互补性；传统交叉验证方法会高估实际应用中的精度；基于研究结果为数据和计算受限条件下的指标选择提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Accurate State-of-Health estimation is essential for safe battery operation and cost-effective maintenance. Although numerous health indicators have been derived from constant-current (CC) and constant-voltage (CV) charging phases, their effectiveness under realistic cross-battery validation remains insufficiently studied. This work addresses this gap through a systematic comparison of CC-only, CV-only, and combined indicator sets using rigorous Leave-One-Battery-Out (LOBO) validation on the NASA battery aging dataset. Four CV-phase indicators and CC phase duration are evaluated individually and in combination. Results show that the combined CC+CV approach achieves the best performance (R2 = 0.874), confirming that CC and CV phases capture complementary degradation information. Moreover, a 119% performance gap is observed between standard 5-fold cross-validation and LOBO validation, indicating that conventional evaluation overestimates practical accuracy. Based on these findings, practical guidelines are provided for indicator selection under data and computational constraints.

</details>


### [85] [An adaptive multi-fuzzy logic model for diagnosing transformer faults using dynamic weight optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23486)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应多模糊逻辑（AMFL）模型，通过集成多种DGA方法与动态权重调整机制，在变压器故障诊断中显著提高了精度、一致性和适应性，优于传统固定权重方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DGA解释方法（如Duval三角、IEC比值、Roger比值、Doernenburg比值和Key Gas）在多故障条件下存在不一致性和精度不足的问题，亟需一种更准确、自适应的诊断方法。

Method: 提出AMFL模型，融合多种DGA方法与模糊逻辑，引入动态权重调整机制：通过迭代评估各方法的诊断性能，基于故障预测精度动态调整权重，并采用反馈优化在每轮循环后重新校准权重，确保收敛到最优解。

Result: 在MATLAB/Simulink中实现并验证，AMFL模型在复杂错误场景下显著提升诊断精度，对新数据集具有更好的适应性。对比分析表明，该方法在精度、一致性和错误检测可靠性上均优于传统固定权重多模糊系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为变压器状态监测提供了一种鲁棒、灵活的诊断工具，支持更准确的资产管理决策。

Abstract: Dissolved gas analysis (DGA) is crucial for diagnosing early power transformer failures. Traditional DGA interpretation methods like Duval Triangle, IEC ratio, Roger ratio, Doernenburg ratio and Key Gas are inconsistent and vary in accuracy, especially for multiple fault conditions. We propose an Adaptive Multi-Fuzzy Logic (AMFL) model integrating multiple DGA methods with fuzzy logic and a dynamic weight adjustment mechanism. Unlike existing approaches with fixed weights, this system iteratively evaluates each method's diagnostic performance, identifies multiple fault types, and adjusts weights based on fault prediction accuracy. A feedback-based optimization recalibrates weights after each cycle to ensure optimal solution convergence. The model, implemented in MATLAB/Simulink, is validated against DGA datasets with known error conditions. Results show the AMFL model significantly improves diagnostic accuracy, especially in complex error scenarios, and enhances adaptability to new datasets. Comparative analysis demonstrates the proposed method outperforms traditional fixed weight multi-fuzzy systems in accuracy, consistency, and reliability of error detection. This work provides a robust, flexible diagnostic tool for transformer condition monitoring and supports more accurate asset management decisions.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Sampling Parameters for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23488)
*Arisrei Lim,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeSAMP是一个通过强化学习为文本到图像扩散模型学习提示条件化、时间步变化的采样参数调度策略框架，在多个评估指标上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型使用大量推理时采样参数（提示、负提示、无分类器引导尺度、噪声调度等），但这些参数通常被手动选择一次并在所有提示和去噪时间步中固定不变，而不同提示和生成阶段可能受益于不同的参数值。

Method: 提出LeSAMP框架，将参数选择建模为强化学习问题：利用大型语言模型根据用户提示生成采样参数的调度策略，并通过人类偏好模型和VLM-as-a-judge的奖励信号进行优化。

Result: 在Flux.1 [dev]和Stable Diffusion 3.5上评估，相比基线，LeSAMP在人类偏好分数上胜率达68.12%，在VLM-as-a-judge上胜率达73.37%，用户研究中胜率达59.46%。

Conclusion: 学习采样参数策略为改进扩散模型输出提供了一种对现有后训练方法的补充途径。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models expose many inference-time sampling parameters, including prompts, negative prompts, classifier-free guidance scales, and noise schedules. These parameters are typically manually chosen once and then held fixed across prompts and denoising timesteps, even though different prompts and stages of generation can benefit from different parameter values. We introduce LeSAMP, a framework for learning prompt-conditioned, timestep-varying sampling parameters. We formulate parameter selection as a reinforcement learning problem: Given a user prompt, a large language model is trained to emit schedules for the chosen sampling parameters. We optimize our model using rewards from human preference models and VLM-as-a-judge. We evaluate our model on Flux.1 [dev] and Stable Diffusion 3.5, and find that compared to baselines, LeSAMP has a win rate of up to 68.12% using human preference scores and 73.37% using VLM-as-a-judge. These gains are validated in a user study where we achieve win rates of up to 59.46% over previous baselines. Our results suggest that learned sampling-parameter policies provide a complementary approach to existing post-training methods for improving diffusion model outputs.

</details>


### [87] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Discovering Periodic Orbits in the Gravitational Three-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23501)
*Nikolaos Kollias,Nikolaos Matzakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，无需初始条件，仅通过稀疏含噪观测数据训练的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）即可恢复三体问题的周期轨道，甚至能发现训练数据中不存在的轨道族。通过固定频率傅里叶特征、百分位自适应细化等技巧，约23-25%的运行收敛到新轨道族。实验表明训练数据源显著影响恢复轨道族的分布，而权重初始化分布则无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌动力学系统（如三体问题）中，周期轨道的定位通常需要接近目标轨道的初始猜测，依赖数值延拓或梯度搜索方法。本文旨在探索能否利用PINNs从稀疏含噪观测数据中直接恢复周期轨道，而无需初始条件或精确初值。

Method: 基于二阶ODE形式的PINNs，结合固定频率傅里叶特征、百分位自适应细化（percentile-based adaptive refinement）以及可训练的缩放参数。训练数据为稀疏含噪观测值，不包含初始条件。通过两个100种子集合的统计实验（χ²检验）分析影响轨道族恢复的因素。

Result: 23%-25%的运行收敛到训练数据中不存在的轨道族。χ²检验表明：训练数据源显著改变恢复轨道族的分布（p < 0.001, Cramér's V = 0.339），而两种权重初始化分布之间无显著差异（p = 0.620, V = 0.094）。网络从拉格朗日数据中恢复了figure-eight轨道（Li-Liao I.A.1类，T*匹配到7位有效数字），从figure-eight数据中恢复了Broucke-Hadjidemetriou-Hénon轨道（δ_T < 10^{-9}）。

Conclusion: PINNs能从稀疏含噪数据中恢复可验证的周期轨道，而非仅似是而非的结果。恢复的轨道可精化为真正的周期解。但PINNs在适定初值问题上仍慢于传统积分器，且未对损失景观进行解析刻画。

Abstract: Locating periodic solutions of chaotic dynamical systems normally requires an initial guess close enough to the target orbit for numerical continuation or gradient-based search to converge. We show that Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) trained on sparse, noisy observations \emph{without} initial conditions recover periodic orbits of the gravitational three-body problem, including orbit families absent from the training data. The method rests on a second-order ODE formulation, fixed-frequency Fourier features, percentile-based adaptive refinement, and a trainable scaling parameter, each validated on forward problems. Across two 100-seed ensembles, $23$--$25\%$ of runs converge to families not present in the training data. We then ask what determines which family emerges. Two $χ^2$ tests give a consistent answer: changing the training data source significantly shifts the distribution of recovered families ($p < 0.001$, Cramér's $V = 0.339$), whereas switching between the two initialization distributions tested does not ($p = 0.620$, $V = 0.094$). The random seed selects which family a given run recovers; the \emph{distribution} the weights are drawn from does not shift the aggregate frequencies, but the training data does. The evidence is empirical: we do not characterize the loss landscape analytically, and PINNs remain slower than conventional integrators on well-posed initial-value problems. What the experiments establish is that the recovered orbits are verifiable rather than merely plausible: the identified ones refine to genuine periodic solutions, a network trained on Lagrange data recovers the figure-eight choreography (Li--Liao class I.A.1, matched to seven significant digits in $T^*$), and one trained on figure-eight data recovers a Broucke--Hadjidemetriou--Hénon orbit closing to $δ_T < 10^{-9}$.

</details>


### [88] [Impute On-Demand: Adaptive Correlated Time Series Imputation for Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23503)
*Zhichen Lai,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang,Dong Gong,Lina Yao,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaCTSi是一种自适应时序插补器，通过结合一次性时序卷积网络、学习型时间-传感器索引表、稀疏空间注意力和相关性加权传感器选择，实现对动态IoT环境中缺失值的准确插补，在五个基准数据集上相比最强基线平均降低MAE 33.1%，并支持传感器子集自适应和资源自适应推理，可部署于包括MCU在内的通用设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTS数据插补方法强调准确性，但缺乏对动态IoT环境的适应性：易受传感器故障影响、无法选择性插补不完整传感器、使用不能适应资源可用性的静态架构。

Method: 结合一次性时序卷积网络（One-shot TCN）与学习型时间-传感器索引表（Learned Time-Sensor Index Table）以解耦时空特征为传感器级嵌入；采用稀疏空间注意力（Sparse Spatial Attention）提取动态空间相关性；使用相关性加权传感器选择（Correlation-Weighted Sensor Selection）选择信息丰富的传感器提供空间上下文。

Result: 与12种基线方法对比，在5个基准数据集（交通、空气质量、轨迹）上及3种适应性场景下，AdaCTSi相比每个数据集上的最强基线平均降低MAE 33.1%。单一训练模型支持传感器子集自适应和资源自适应推理，内存占用小，可部署于包括MCU在内的通用计算设备。

Conclusion: AdaCTSi有效解决了现有方法在动态IoT环境中的适应性问题，在保持高精度的同时实现了传感器子集和资源自适应的灵活推理，且具有低内存开销的实用部署优势。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) applications generate vast amounts of Correlated Time Series (CTS) data that often contain missing values and require imputation. Existing methods emphasize accuracy but often lack adaptability to changing IoT environments: they are vulnerable to sensor failures, cannot selectively impute only incomplete sensors, and use static architectures that do not adapt to resource availability. To address these limitations, we propose AdaCTSi, an adaptive CTS imputer for changing environments. AdaCTSi combines a One-shot Temporal Convolutional Network with a Learned Time-Sensor Index Table to extract and decouple complex spatio-temporal features into sensor-wise embeddings, enabling adaptation to varying sensor subsets. Sparse Spatial Attention efficiently extracts dynamic spatial correlations, while Correlation-Weighted Sensor Selection selects informative sensors to provide sufficient spatial context. Experiments with twelve baseline methods, three adaptability scenarios, and five benchmark datasets covering traffic, air quality, and trajectory data show that AdaCTSi reduces MAE by an average of 33.1% relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. A single trained model supports sensor-subset and resource-adaptive inference, and its modest memory footprint enables deployment on commodity computing devices, including MCUs.

</details>


### [89] [Topological Data Analysis and Graph-Theoretic Approaches for Tennis Match Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23509)
*Jake Schwaderer,Alexander Bastien,Omid Khormali,Alejandro Navarrete,Mia Pesavento,Angelika Elderbrook*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于拓扑数据分析和图神经网络的网球比赛结果预测方法，在2000-2025年ATP单打比赛中，随机森林模型达到66.2%准确率(AUC=0.719)，其中排名特征贡献36.3%，中心性特征25.5%，拓扑特征24.0%；仅使用拓扑特征时仍可达63.56%准确率。第二种方法采用改进的Katz相似性指数，准确率为62.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑数据分析和图论方法在网球比赛结果预测中的应用，验证网络拓扑结构能否捕捉选手间的竞争关系，并系统比较不同拓扑摘要方法在体育分析中的表现。

Method: 方法一：构建选手竞争网络，采用下星滤波提取拓扑特征，结合四种摘要方法(VAB、HNAV、HWNAV、OW-HNPV)和修正带深度分析，通过自我图近似和三角形消除等算法优化处理约6.6万场比赛，使用随机森林模型整合拓扑、图论和排名特征。方法二：使用时序边权重的改进Katz相似性指数进行预测。

Result: 随机森林模型准确率66.2%(AUC=0.719)；特征重要性：排名36.3%、中心性25.5%、TDA特征24.0%；仅拓扑特征模型准确率63.56%；改进Katz方法准确率62.48%。

Conclusion: 本文首次将下星滤波应用于网球预测，系统比较了四种拓扑摘要方法，证明TDA可仅凭网络拓扑实现高于随机的预测，并与传统特征组合时提供互补信息。

Abstract: We present two approaches for predicting tennis match outcomes using topological data analysis and graph theory on ATP singles matches from 2000-2025. The first method applies lower-star filtration to player competitive networks, extracting topological features through persistent homology using four summary methods (VAB, HNAV, HWNAV, OW-HNPV) combined with Modified Band Depth analysis. Algorithmic optimizations including ego graph approximations and triangle elimination enable analysis of about 66k matches. Our Random Forest model achieves 66.2% accuracy (AUC = 0.719) using topological, graph-theoretic, and ranking features. Feature importance analysis reveals that rankings contribute 36.3%, centralities 25.5%, and TDA features 24.0%, with topological features providing complementary signal. When rankings are unavailable, the topology-only model maintains 63.56% accuracy, demonstrating that network-derived features alone capture meaningful competitive structure. The second method uses a modified Katz similarity index with temporal edge weighting, achieving 62.48% accuracy on held-out test data. This work represents the first application of lower-star filtration to tennis prediction, provides systematic comparison of four topological summary methods in sports analytics, and demonstrates that TDA can achieve above-chance prediction using network topology alone while providing additional value when combined with traditional features.

</details>


### [90] [Chamaileon: Cross-Context Binder Design with Contextualized Modeling and Mixed Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23518)
*Hengyuan Cao,Shizhuo Cheng,Mingxuan Liu,Weicheng Huang,Yunhong Lu,Chenxi Cai,Yan Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chamaileon提出了一种统一的多目标/多状态蛋白结合剂设计框架，通过上下文感知的序列-结构共建模（I3CD训练范式）和混合路径采样（MoPS）策略，在CROSS基准上有效生成适应不同构象景观和多目标需求的序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白结合剂设计方法局限于单目标、单状态假设，无法建模多目标或多状态相互作用，限制了面向高级功能蛋白设计的能力。

Method: 提出Chamaileon框架，将问题形式化为跨上下文结合景观建模；采用上下文内复合物共设计（I3CD）训练范式实现上下文感知的序列-结构共建模；推理时使用混合路径采样（MoPS）策略，在不同上下文中优化单一序列并缓解高质量多构象配对数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在新构建的CROSS基准上，Chamaileon有效生成适应多样构象景观和多目标需求的序列。

Conclusion: Chamaileon通过统一多目标/多状态的蛋白结合剂设计框架，突破了单目标/单状态的局限，为功能导向的蛋白设计提供了新范式，且方法具有可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative models has unlocked new potentials in protein binder design, a pivotal task in structural biology, by facilitating end-to-end generation via joint sequence-structure modeling or hallucination. However, existing approaches are predominantly implemented under a single-target, single-state assumption, limiting their ability to model multi-target or multi-state interactions required for advanced function-oriented protein design. Here, we introduce Chamaileon, which unifies multi-target and multi-state binder design by formulating the problem as cross-context binding landscape modeling. The framework is underpinned by a training paradigm termed In-Context Complex Co-Design (I3CD) for context-aware sequence-structure co-modeling. During inference, we employ Mixture-of-Paths Sampling (MoPS), a scalable strategy that optimizes a single sequence across contexts while alleviating the scarcity of high-quality multi-conformational paired data. Extensive evaluation on our newly constructed benchmark, CROSS, demonstrates that Chamaileon effectively generates sequences adaptable to diverse conformational landscapes and multi-target requirements. The code is available on https://github.com/caohengyuan/Chamaileon.

</details>


### [91] [Neonatal Hypoxic-ischaemic Encephalopathy Classification from the EEG and HRV Signals Using a Conformer based Masked Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23554)
*Shuwen Yu,William P Marnane,Geraldine B. Boylan,Gordon Lightbody*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAEConformer框架，结合Conformer架构与掩码自编码器范式，在未标注的EEG和HRV信号上进行大规模自监督表征学习。通过多分辨率STFT损失增强重建质量，在6030小时EEG和4868小时HRV数据上预训练后，在HIE严重程度分类任务中EEG模型达到97.19% AUC（二分类），HRV模型达到82.42% AUC，均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督学习方法依赖大规模标注数据，而EEG和HRV等生理信号的标注成本高昂；同时现有自监督方法在捕获生理时间序列的局部时序模式和长程上下文依赖方面存在不足，亟需一种能够从无标注数据中有效学习鲁棒、可迁移表征的框架。

Method: 提出MAEConformer框架，将Conformer架构（结合卷积与Transformer自注意力）与掩码自编码器预训练范式相结合；引入多分辨率短时傅里叶变换(MR-STFT)损失，使模型在重建过程中同时学习多尺度时域和频域特征；分别在6,030小时EEG和4,868小时HRV无标注数据上独立预训练EEG和HRV专用模型，再迁移到下游标注任务进行微调。

Result: 在EEG-HIE二分类任务中达到97.19% AUC，四分类达96.56% AUC，优于多种监督和自监督基线；HRV-HIE分类任务达82.42% AUC，超过基于Transformer的自监督和基于卷积的监督基线；验证了跨生理模态的强迁移性和数据效率。

Conclusion: MAEConformer能够有效从无标注的EEG和HRV信号中学习鲁棒且可迁移的表征，为多模态生理信号的自监督预训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the MAEConformer, a novel self-supervised learning framework that combines the Conformer architecture with the Masked Autoencoder (MAE) paradigm for large-scale representation learning from unlabelled electroencephalography (EEG) and heart rate variability (HRV) signals. By integrating convolutional operations with Transformer-based self-attention, MAEConformer effectively captures both local temporal patterns and long-range contextual dependencies in physiological time series. To enhance reconstruction fidelity and representation quality, a multi-resolution short-time Fourier transform (MR-STFT) loss is incorporated alongside the reconstruction objective, enabling the model to jointly learn temporal and spectral characteristics across multiple scales. Modality-specific EEG and HRV MAEConformer models were pretrained on 6,030h and 4,868h of unlabelled recordings, respectively, and subsequently transferred to expert-annotated downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned representations provide strong transferability and data efficiency. In EEG-based hypoxic ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) severity classification, the pretrained MAE-EEG model achieved test AUCs of 97.19% and 96.56% for binary and four-class classification tasks, respectively, outperforming a range of state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines. On the HRV-based HIE severity classification task, MAE-HRV achieved a test AUC of 82.42%, surpassing both self-supervised Transformer-based and supervised convolutional baselines. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of MAEConformer for learning robust and transferable representations across multiple physiological modalities.

</details>


### [92] [Random Forest-Based Prediction of Bone Volume Fraction and Fracture Position from S-Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23563)
*Jianhe Li,Jinsui Meng,Yida Zhao,Zihe Wang,Liaoran Sun,Tao Shan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型，用于预测骨体积分数（BVF）和骨折位置，并通过九天线微波扫描系统与骨模仿真体实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在骨质量评估和骨折预测方面存在局限性，需要一种非侵入性、准确且高效的骨组织特性评估方法。

Method: 设计并制造了九天线微波扫描系统以获取多通道S参数数据，开发骨模仿真体，构建基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型进行BVF和骨折位置预测。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明所提方法在预测骨体积分数和骨折位置方面均有效。

Conclusion: 基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型能够有效预测BVF和骨折位置，为骨组织健康评估提供了一种新的非侵入性技术手段。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method for predicting bone volume fraction (BVF) and fracture position by constructing a random forest model based on multichannel S-parameters. A nine-antenna microwave scanning system is designed and fabricated to acquire the multichannel S-parameter data. Bone-mimicking phantoms are developed, and corresponding experiments are conducted to validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Both synthetic and experimental results demonstrate the validity of the method.

</details>


### [93] [MS-GPT: Rethinking MS/MS De Novo Structure Elucidation as Spectrum-Induced Posterior Querying of a Molecule-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23607)
*Xin Zhao,Yumin Liu,Zhuo Li,Weichu Zheng,Feng Zhu,Xiaokang Yang,Yaohui Jin,Yanyan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-GPT是一种通过串联质谱进行分子结构解析的新方法，它将指纹介导的从头解析重新表述为条件分子语言模型的谱诱导后验查询，利用主动比特密度校准和LoRA适配器解决训练-推理不匹配问题，在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym上达到了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从头解析方法通常先预测分子指纹再从指纹解码结构，但存在训练-推理不匹配问题：解码器在干净指纹上训练，推理时却面对从噪声谱诱导的后验指纹（通常被阈值化压缩为单一向量）。作者希望弥合这一差距，使模型能更好利用谱图信息进行分子结构解析。

Method: MS-GPT将条件分子语言模型（如GPT风格）应用于指纹和分子式条件，通过主动比特密度校准将谱诱导后验转化为围绕标准指纹的一系列指纹查询；该带内采样的候选分子通过生成频率共识进行池化和排序；此外采用轻量级LoRA适配器缓解领域特定后验偏差，同时保留预训练分子先验。

Result: 在NPLIB1上Top-1/Top-10精确匹配准确率达29.8%/41.1%，在MassSpecGym上达23.9%/28.7%，均达到新的最优水平。候选池扩展表明高效的自回归分子生成能以较小的额外推理成本持续提升召回率。

Conclusion: MS-GPT通过后验查询策略有效解决了指纹介导的从头解析中的训练-推理不匹配问题，结合LoRA微调保留了强大的分子先验，在标准基准上取得了显著优于现有方法的性能，并展示了扩展推理计算量持续改进结果的可扩展性。

Abstract: Molecular structure elucidation from tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) is a central inverse problem in analytical chemistry. Most existing approaches to MS/MS identification remain tied to reference libraries or predefined candidate sets, whereas de novo methods aim to generate structures directly from spectra. A common de novo route predicts a molecular fingerprint from the spectrum and then decodes structures from it, enabling decoder pretraining on large molecule-only corpora. However, this paradigm creates a training-inference mismatch: the decoder is trained on oracle fingerprints computed from molecules, but at inference it is queried with a noisy spectrum-induced fingerprint posterior that is typically collapsed to a single thresholded fingerprint. We introduce MS-GPT, which recasts fingerprint-mediated de novo elucidation as spectrum-induced posterior querying of a conditional molecule-language model. MS-GPT conditions a molecule-language model on fingerprints and formulas, then converts the spectrum-induced posterior into a band of fingerprint queries near the oracle-fingerprint manifold through active-bit density calibration. Candidates sampled across this band are pooled and ranked by generation-frequency consensus. A lightweight LoRA adapter further mitigates domain-specific posterior bias while preserving the pretrained molecular prior. On NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym, MS-GPT sets a new state of the art, reaching Top-1/Top-10 exact-match accuracy of 29.8\%/41.1\% and 23.9\%/28.7\%, respectively. Candidate-pool scaling shows that efficient autoregressive molecular generation continues to improve recall with a little additional inference cost. The source code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/VIKI623/MS-GPT.

</details>


### [94] [Restoration Flow Matching-Based Channel Refinement and Equalization Correction for MIMO Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23615)
*Wenkai Liu,Nan Ma,Jianqiao Chen,Xiaodong Xu,Meixia Tao,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MIMO语义通信中CSI不完美和均衡失配导致语义重建质量下降的问题，提出统一的基于恢复流匹配(RFM)的框架，包含信道RFM(CRFM)和语义RFM(SRFM)两个模块，分别用于信道精化和均衡校正，通过双锚点扰动训练策略增强鲁棒性，以更少采样步骤实现信道估计和语义重建质量的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MIMO语义通信中，不完美的信道状态信息(CSI)和均衡失配会严重降低语义重建质量，现有方法难以有效解决这两个级联逆问题。

Method: 提出统一的RFM框架，包含CRFM模块用于精化粗糙信道以提升信道估计精度，以及SRFM模块用于校正后均衡潜在空间中的残差失真。将信道估计和均衡两个级联逆问题建模为统一的条件恢复任务，通过学习的条件速度场引导扰动分布向目标分布演化；提出双锚点扰动训练策略联合学习近流形精化和大误差校正；推理时使用少步确定性ODE求解器。

Result: 在MIMO信道和视觉语义传输任务上的实验表明，所提方案在信道估计和语义重建质量的关键指标上均有提升，且相比基于扩散的生成基线方法需要更少的采样步骤。

Conclusion: 提出的RFM框架有效解决了MIMO语义通信中CSI不完美和均衡失配导致的语义重建退化问题，且具有更高的采样效率。

Abstract: In multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) semantic communication, imperfect channel state information (CSI) and equalization mismatch can seriously degrade semantic reconstruction quality. To address this issue, we propose a unified restoration flow matching (RFM)-based framework for channel refinement and equalization correction. Specifically, the channel RFM (CRFM) module is developed to refine the coarse channel, thereby improving channel estimation accuracy. Based on the refined channel, the developed semantic RFM (SRFM) module is employed to correct the residual distortions in the post-equalization latent space. The key idea is to formulate the two cascaded inverse problems of channel estimation and equalization as the unified conditional restoration task, in which the learned conditional velocity field guides the perturbed distribution towards the target distribution. To enhance the robustness of these two modules under various distortion conditions, we develop a dual-anchor perturbation training strategy that jointly learns near-manifold refinement and large-error correction, and implement inference through a few-step deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE) solver. Extensive experiments on MIMO channels and visual semantic transmission tasks demonstrate that the proposed scheme improves key metrics for channel estimation and semantic reconstruction quality. Moreover, compared with representative diffusion-based generative baselines, the proposed method requires fewer sampling steps.

</details>


### [95] [Optimal Reward Shaping: Autonomous Car Parking Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23617)
*Emre Özkaya,Nicolas R. Gauger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非完整约束下模型无关强化学习的奖励函数设计难题，提出了一种参数化奖励塑形框架，包含覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化和对齐回合终止机制，并采用基于代理的贝叶斯优化对环境奖励参数和算法超参数进行联合元优化，显著提升了自主平行泊车任务的成功率和轨迹平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整约束下模型无关强化学习的奖励函数设计常导致严重的局部最小值问题（如策略瘫痪或过度保守的避障），现有方法难以有效解决。

Method: 提出参数化奖励塑形框架，包含三项核心机制：覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化、对齐回合终止机制；并采用基于代理的贝叶斯优化对环境奖励参数和算法超参数进行联合元优化。

Result: 联合优化的DQN代理解决了特征性控制失效模式，在成功率和轨迹平滑度上均显著优于未校准基线。

Conclusion: 环境奖励参数与算法超参数存在深度相互依赖关系，需要联合元优化才能实现稳定收敛。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions for model-free reinforcement learning under non-holonomic constraints remains a persistent challenge, often resulting in severe local minima such as policy paralysis or over-conservative hazard avoidance. In this work, we present a parameterized reward shaping framework featuring coverage-gated alignment feedback, drive-direction switch regularization, and an aligned episode termination mechanism evaluated on an autonomous parallel parking task. Crucially, we show that environmental reward parameters and algorithmic hyperparameters are deeply co-dependent, requiring joint meta-optimization to achieve stable convergence. By employing surrogate-based Bayesian optimization, our co-optimized Deep Q-Network (DQN) agent resolves characteristic control failure modes, significantly outperforming uncalibrated baselines across both success rate and trajectory smoothness.

</details>


### [96] [Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA):TailoredAttentionMattersfor Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23634)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分伊辛注意力（VIA），通过在softmax归一化中融入伊辛相互作用模型，将注意力机制从独立项排序重新定义为交互实体的集体状态，并在逆合成反应中心预测任务上显著优于标准softmax注意力，表明科学问题应使用契合领域内在结构的定制化注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制（softmax）假设各元素独立，缺乏对交互实体的结构化耦合建模能力。尽管工业界追求长上下文的稀疏高效，但科学任务（如化学逆合成）中实体间的约束关系至关重要，因此需要为这些问题设计定制化的注意力机制。

Method: 提出Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA)，在softmax归一化基础上融合伊辛模型（Ising model），通过变分平均场推断学习可训练的成对耦合参数，使注意力模式从孤立项排序变为交互实体的集体状态。

Result: 在逆合成反应中心预测任务上，VIA在多种模型变体下一致且显著优于标准softmax注意力，并经过机制分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 科学问题的最优解并非通用的高效注意力，而是与领域内在结构对齐的定制化注意力。VIA为此提供了理论扎实且实验验证了的实现范例。

Abstract: Attention enables context modeling via query-key scoring with softmax normalization. Driven by industrial long-context demands, mainstream research has converged toward sparsity and efficiency--yet softmax's independence assumption persists. For scientific tasks unburdened by long-token constraints, however, richer structured coupling may often be essential, making tailored attention both viable and more appropriate. To this end, we propose Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA), which augments softmax normalization with an interacting Ising model; attention patterns emerge from learnable pairwise couplings via variational mean-field inference, redefining attention from a ranking over isolated items to a collective state over interacting entities. We instantiate VIA on retrosynthesis reaction center prediction, a task inherently governed by cooperative bond-breaking constraints. Comprehensive experiments across model variants, coupled with mechanistic analyses, demonstrate that VIA consistently and substantially outperforms standard softmax attention. More broadly, our findings suggest that for scientific problems, the optimal solution is not general-purpose efficiency, but appropriately tailored attention aligned with intrinsic domain structure. This work provides a theoretically grounded and empirically validated instantiation of this paradigm.

</details>


### [97] [CALMRec: Causally Aligned Language Memory for Long-Horizon Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23647)
*Gengyu Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的长期推荐框架，利用冻结的多模态语言模型将物品内容和反馈转化为基于证据的语义原子，并分别维护短期、长期和曝光记忆。通过倾向性加权更新减少曝光偏差，保守离线批评者基于行为支持约束对候选集重排序，解释仅使用关键证据原子并通过反事实删除验证。在电商、新闻和短视频环境下，该方法相比最强基线在折扣长期价值上分别提升了6.1%、7.6%和6.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统将用户的持久偏好、瞬时意图和曝光诱导行为混为一个用户画像，导致推荐易受反馈循环影响：重复曝光被误认为偏好、即时点击主导延迟满足、流畅的解释不一定反映排序决策。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的多模态语言模型将物品内容和用户反馈转化为证据基础的语义原子；2) 分别维护短期、长期和曝光记忆三种独立记忆；3) 倾向性加权更新减少曝光偏差；4) 保守离线批评者基于行为支持约束对候选集重排序；5) 解释仅使用影响性证据原子并通过反事实删除验证。

Result: 在电商、新闻和短视频环境中，相比最强基线在折扣长期价值上分别提升6.1%、7.6%和6.7%。二十种子配对消融实验显示，去除倾向性校正后价值显著下降0.739±0.191，去除保守支持正则化后下降0.523±0.234。冻结指令语言模型在保留语义原子NDCG上比TF-IDF提升超过一倍。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分离记忆、减少曝光偏差和引入行为支持约束，有效解决了LLM推荐中的反馈循环问题，显著提升了长期推荐价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can summarize heterogeneous user evidence in natural language, but current LLM recommenders often collapse enduring preferences, transient intent, and exposure-induced behavior into one profile. This makes recommendation vulnerable to feedback loops: repeated exposure is mistaken for preference, immediate clicks dominate delayed satisfaction, and fluent explanations need not reflect the ranking decision. We propose our method, a model-agnostic framework for long-horizon recommendation. Our method uses a frozen multimodal language model to convert item content and feedback into evidence-grounded semantic atoms, then maintains separate short-term, long-term, and exposure memories. Propensity-weighted updates reduce policy-induced exposure bias, while a conservative offline critic reranks candidates for delayed satisfaction under a behavior-support constraint. Explanations use only influential evidence atoms and are checked by counterfactual deletion. We provide an identification result and evaluate the framework in e-commerce-like, news-like, and short-video-like environments. Across ten seeds, our method improves discounted long-term value over the strongest alternative by 6.1%, 7.6%, and 6.7%, respectively. Twenty-seed paired ablations show significant value drops after removing propensity correction (0.739 +/- 0.191) or conservative support regularization (0.523 +/- 0.234). A frozen instruction language model also more than doubles semantic-atom NDCG over TF-IDF on a held-out paraphrase benchmark.

</details>


### [98] [DP-IVON-Gradsq: Differentially Private Squared-Gradient Improved Variational Online Newton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23649)
*Nour Jamoussi,Ikram Dridi,Giuseppe Serra,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-IVON-Gradsq，一种结合差分隐私与贝叶斯变分学习的私有优化器，通过在IVON优化器中引入噪声校正的平方梯度估计器来构建曲率信息，减少后验采样噪声与隐私噪声的交互。实验表明，在弱到中等隐私约束（ε较大）下具有竞争力，但在强隐私约束下性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私与贝叶斯深度学习难以结合，因为隐私噪声会与贝叶斯后验采样引入的随机性相互干扰，需要设计一种能够有效协调这两种噪声源的私有优化方法。

Method: 基于IVON（Improved Variational Online Newton）优化器，提出DP-IVON-Gradsq：利用噪声校正的平方梯度估计器从私有化梯度中构建曲率估计，减少后验采样噪声与隐私噪声的直接交互，同时保持IVON类似Adam的计算效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，与DP-SGD和DP-Adam对比，DP-IVON-Gradsq在弱到中等隐私约束（大到中等ε值）下表现具有竞争力，但在强隐私约束（小ε值）下性能退化。

Conclusion: DP-IVON-Gradsq在中等隐私保护需求场景下是有效的私有贝叶斯学习方法，但在强隐私保护场景下仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Differential privacy provides formal privacy guarantees for training neural networks on sensitive data, while Bayesian deep learning offers a principled framework for uncertainty-aware prediction. Combining these two objectives remains challenging, as privacy noise can interact with the stochasticity introduced by Bayesian posterior sampling. In this work, we investigate differentially private variational Bayesian learning through the Improved Variational Online Newton (IVON) optimizer. We introduce DP-IVON-Gradsq, a private variant of IVON. The proposed method constructs its curvature estimate from the privatized gradient using a noise-corrected squared-gradient estimator, reducing the direct interaction between posterior-sampling noise and privacy noise while preserving the Adam-like computational efficiency of IVON. We evaluate DP-IVON-Gradsq on CIFAR-10 against the standard private optimizers DP-SGD and DP-Adam over a range of privacy budgets. The results show that DP-IVON-Gradsq is competitive under weak-to-moderate privacy constraints, i.e., large-to-moderate values of $\varepsilon$, while degrading under strong privacy. Code is available at https://github.com/NourJamoussi/DP-IVON-Gradsq.git.

</details>


### [99] [Breaking the Total Variance Barrier: Sharp Sample Complexity for Linear Heteroscedastic Bandits with Fixed Action Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23679)
*Heyang Zhao,Tianyuan Jin,Weixin Wang,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Pan Xu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对异方差噪声下的随机线性Bandit问题，提出了一种方差自适应的主动探索算法VAEE，首次打破了$\sqrt{\Lambda}$的复杂度依赖，实现了近乎调和平均的简单遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异方差噪声Bandit工作的遗憾界依赖于$\sqrt{\Lambda}$，但$\Lambda$在噪声半数为零时仍保持不变，说明该依赖并非最优。本文旨在打破这一界限，实现更紧的依赖关系。

Method: 提出VAEE算法：在候选动作集上主动探索最大化信息增益的动作；对于有限动作集，提出基于G-optimal设计的方差感知变体进行探索。

Result: VAEE实现了近乎调和平均依赖的简单遗憾界；提出了近乎匹配的下界，表明调和平均依赖率无法避免。

Conclusion: 这是首个在异方差噪声随机线性Bandit中打破$\sqrt{\Lambda}$屏障的工作，调和平均依赖率是该设定下的本质最优。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed increasing interests in tackling heteroscedastic noise in bandits and reinforcement learning. In these works, the cumulative variance of the noise $Λ= \sum_{t=1}^T σ_t^2$, where $σ_t^2$ is the variance of the noise at round $t$, is used to characterize the statistical complexity of the problem, yielding \emph{simple regret} bounds of order $\tilde{\cal{O}}(d \sqrt{Λ/ T^2})$ for $d$-dimensional linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise. However, with a closer look, $Λ$ remains the same order even if the noise is close to zero at half of the rounds, which indicates that the $Λ$-dependence is not optimal. In this paper, we revisit the stochastic linear bandit problem with heteroscedastic noise, where the action set is prefixed throughout the learning process. We propose a novel variance-adaptive algorithm \texttt{VAEE} (Variance-Aware Exploration with Elimination) for large action set, which actively explores actions that maximizes the information gain among a candidate set of actions that are not eliminated. With the active-exploration strategy, we show that \texttt{VAEE} achieves a \emph{simple regret} with a nearly \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate. For finitely many actions, we propose a variance-aware variant of G-optimal design based exploration, which achieves a simple regret with sharper dependence on $d$. We also establish a nearly matching lower bound for the fixed action set setting indicating that \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate is unavoidable. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that breaks the $\sqrtΛ$ barrier for stochastic linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise.

</details>


### [100] [Extreme Volatility Warning under Label Scarcity via Multi-Source Anomaly Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23682)
*Jin Qian,Zhangzhi Xiong,Mingrui Li,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融风险预警中标签稀缺（CSI 300仅80个正样本）的问题，提出AAMSF半监督框架：利用Isolation Forest对市场指标、GDELT事件、中英文新闻等多源信号进行异常评分，再通过轻量级Ridge融合。在测试集上AUC-ROC达0.680，优于监督神经网络（0.588）和无监督基线（0.630）。消融实验发现GDELT与国内财经新闻互补，英文媒体反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端市场波动预警事件稀少（CSI 300中791个训练日仅约80个正样本）、非平稳且常由外生信息冲击触发，导致有监督多源融合模型过参数化不稳定（如100K参数的HTSF表现不佳），因此需要针对低标签场景设计更鲁棒的预警方法。

Method: 提出AAMSF半监督框架：先用Isolation Forest对市场指标、GDELT事件、中文金融新闻和英文媒体分别计算异常分数，再用轻量级Ridge回归融合这些异常分数，无需大量标注数据。T-AAMSF则是其时序扩展，支持多日异常累积。

Result: 在CSI 300（2018-2023）上，AAMSF的测试AUC-ROC为0.680，优于最强无监督基线（0.630）和神经网络基线（0.588）；T-AAMSF将PR-AUC提升至0.291。消融实验显示GDELT和国内财经新闻贡献互补风险信号，而英文媒体始终降低性能，且学习权重在验证噪声下不可靠。

Conclusion: 在标签稀缺的金融风险预警中，鲁棒的异常几何结构和信号源可靠性比有监督表示能力更重要，应优先选择可靠信号源并采用轻量级融合策略。

Abstract: Early warning of extreme market volatility is central to financial risk management, but actionable events are rare, nonstationary, and often triggered by exogenous information shocks. In our CSI~300 setting, only $\sim$80 positive samples are observed across 791 training days, making heavily supervised multi-source models unstable. We first analyze a 100K-parameter hierarchical text-signal fusion model (HTSF) and find that added parameterization hurts in this low-label regime. Motivated by this failure, we propose \textbf{AAMSF} (Anomaly-Augmented Multi-Signal Fusion), a semisupervised framework that combines Isolation Forest anomaly scores over market indicators, GDELT events, Chinese financial news, and English media with lightweight Ridge score fusion. We further introduce \textbf{T-AAMSF}, a temporal extension for multi-day anomaly accumulation. On CSI~300 (2018--2023), AAMSF achieves test AUC-ROC \textbf{0.680}, outperforming the strongest unsupervised baseline (0.630) and neural baseline (0.588), while T-AAMSF improves PR-AUC to 0.291. Ablations reveal strong source asymmetry: GDELT and domestic financial news provide complementary risk signals, whereas English media consistently reduces performance, and learned weighting is unreliable under validation noise. These results suggest an empirical design principle for label-scarce financial risk warning: robust anomaly geometry and source reliability can matter more than supervised representation capacity.

</details>


### [101] [The Intruder Threshold: A Spectral Law for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23711)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了LoRA微调中入侵维度出现的每层临界更新强度s*，通过矩形spiked-deformation变换从预训练权重谱直接计算，并在18个适配器、9840层扫描中验证了该理论对遗忘起点的预测能力。结合阈值预算规则，无需验证即可减少最脆弱模型62%的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调会引入入侵维度（新的主导奇异向量），导致灾难性遗忘，但此前缺乏理论来逐层预测入侵维度何时出现。

Method: 通过矩形spiked-deformation变换和精确的久期方程表征，从预训练权重W的谱中直接计算每层临界更新强度s*，无需拟合参数。

Result: 在4个稠密Transformer家族、状态空间模型、混合专家模型和编码器-解码器（共18个适配器、9840层扫描）中，该定律在82%的层上将经验阈值定位在2倍因子内，区分入侵层与非入侵层的平均AUC为0.89；结合两种边缘评估可达98%，外部适配器上为0.997。

Conclusion: 临界更新强度理论有效解释了LoRA与全量微调的非对称性（全量微调更新远低于每层阈值），且基于阈值的spike预算规则（仅需一次SVD、无需验证）可将最脆弱模型遗忘降低62%且不损失任务性能。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning can create intruder dimensions: new leading singular vectors of the updated weight matrix $W+BA$ that are nearly orthogonal to all pretrained singular vectors and that drive catastrophic forgetting. Since their discovery, no theory has predicted, layer by layer on measured spectra, when they appear. We derive a per-layer critical update strength $s^\ast=\barθ/(γσ_1(BA))$, computed from the measured spectrum of $W$ alone through the rectangular spiked-deformation transform, together with an exact secular-equation characterization of the updated spectrum, with no fitted parameters. In a pre-specified study spanning four dense Transformer families, a state-space model, a mixture-of-experts model, and an encoder-decoder (18 adapters, 9{,}840 layer scans), the law localizes the empirical threshold within a factor of two on $82\%$ of layers, separates intruder-bearing from intruder-free layers at deployment with a mean AUC of $0.89$, holds unchanged on six third-party adapters, and predicts where WikiText-2 perplexity begins to degrade; a combination of the two pre-specified edge evaluations reaches $98\%$ and is confirmed out-of-bag on the external adapters ($0.997$). Full fine-tuning disperses its update far below the threshold of every layer, which resolves the asymmetry between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Norm-matched interventions confirm that threshold-crossing layers, rather than update magnitude, carry the forgetting, and a spike-budget rule derived from the thresholds, requiring one SVD and no validation sweeps, reduces forgetting by $62\%$ on the most fragile model at no task cost.

</details>


### [102] [Outcome-Confounded Local Supervision in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23731)
*Guoqing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy蒸馏中，师生在局部token上的一致/分歧与最终答案正确性之间存在混淆（尤其是“失败上的一致”占比极高），并通过诊断实验揭示局部信号+轨迹级结果无法定位失败根源，强调了引入位置信息（如过程标签、教师续写）的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy蒸馏中，教师提供的逐token似然被局部解读：一致被认为安全可模仿，分歧被认为指示错误。但作者认为这两种解读都受到完成轨迹的结果（正确/错误）的混淆，需要系统性地诊断这种混淆。

Method: 提出了一个结合逐点师生分歧与最终答案正确性的交叉诊断框架，将token分为四类：安全模仿、生产性分歧、有害分歧、失败上的一致。在数学推理任务上，使用Qwen3-8B/32B和Qwen2.5-7B/32B师生对进行8个种子的实验，并通过阈值、序列级、格式、截断等多种审计验证结果稳健性。

Result: 失败上的一致（agreement-on-failure）在Qwen3对中占响应token总量的67.84%，在Qwen2.5对中占67.68%。即使在教师四次独立尝试都正确的prompt上，学生准确率升至86.91%，但失败上的一致仍占14.76%。三种基于可用信号的训练探针（模仿、掩码、对比）均未能一致地减少失败上的一致。

Conclusion: 局部分歧+轨迹级结果无法定位一条失败轨迹在何处变得不可挽回，需要额外的位置信息（如过程标签、教师从学生前缀续写、token级对齐）来克服这一限制。本文贡献是诊断性而非提出新训练方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories while a teacher supplies dense token-level likelihoods at student-visited prefixes. These likelihoods are often read locally: agreement appears safe to imitate, whereas disagreement appears to identify an error. We show that both readings are confounded by the outcome of the completed trajectory. We introduce an outcome-resolved diagnostic that crosses pointwise teacher-student divergence with final-answer correctness, separating safe imitation, productive divergence, harmful divergence, and agreement-on-failure. In an eight-seed mathematical-reasoning study with a Qwen3-8B student and Qwen3-32B teacher, agreement-on-failure constitutes 67.84% of pooled response-token mass; with a Qwen2.5-7B/32B pair it remains 67.68%. The result persists across threshold, sequence-level, format, and truncation audits. Even on prompts that the Qwen3 teacher solves in all four independent attempts, student accuracy rises to 86.91% but agreement-on-failure remains 14.76%. We then run three matched training probes that use the available signals to imitate, mask, or contrast whole trajectories; none consistently reduces agreement-on-failure. The result points to a localization limitation: local divergence paired with a trajectory-level outcome does not identify where a failed trajectory became unrecoverable. Addressing this limitation requires additional positional information, such as process labels, teacher continuations from student prefixes, or token-level alignment across rollouts. Our contribution is therefore diagnostic rather than a new training method.

</details>


### [103] [Source-Free Controlled Adaptation of Teachers for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23735)
*Anurag Roy,Riddhiman Moulick,Vinay Kumar Verma,Saptarshi Ghosh,Abir Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种动态调整动量值的受控教师适应方法，结合源预训练模型的类别原型估计，在不访问源数据的情况下实现持续测试时自适应，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTTA方法使用固定的高动量值更新教师网络，在测试数据分布变化时会导致模型漂移，且许多方法需要访问源数据，限制了实用性。

Method: 1) 提出受控教师适应方法，根据输入数据质量动态设置动量值；2) 从源预训练模型估计类别原型，用于对齐目标数据；3) 整个流程无需源数据或统计信息，实现无源自适应。

Result: 在基准数据集上，该方法优于多种需要访问源数据的最先进自适应框架。

Conclusion: 通过动态动量控制和类别原型对齐，可以在无源条件下有效缓解持续测试时自适应中的模型漂移问题。

Abstract: In many real-world scenarios, encountering continual shifts in domain during inference is very common. Consequently, continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) techniques leveraging a teacher-student framework have gained prominence, allowing models to adapt continuously even after deployment. In such a framework, a weight-averaged mean teacher is used to produce pseudo-labels from test data for self-training. The mean teacher gets updated as an exponential moving average of the student parameters using a high value of momentum that is kept fixed even if different distributions of test data are encountered. To combat the resulting drift of the model, we propose a novel controlled teacher adaptation methodology that dynamically sets a proper momentum value depending on the quality of the incoming data. Additionally, we estimate class prototypes from the source pretrained model to help align the target data as they come in. Importantly, our method does not require access to source data or its statistics at any stage of the pipeline, making it truly source-free. We perform extensive experiments on benchmark datasets to demonstrate that our approach outperforms different state-of-the-art adaptation frameworks, many of which require access to source data.

</details>


### [104] [Soft-Constrained Optimization of Latent Space in Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23751)
*Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于熵约束（EC）和权重过滤方法的VAE训练框架，通过软约束优化同时提升潜在变量的编码容量和可解缠性，在dSprites和MNIST数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE中KL正则化强度与潜在变量编码容量、可解缠性之间存在矛盾：弱化KL正则化可提高编码容量但损害解缠性，强化正则化则会导致潜在变量被完全剪枝。现有方法难以同时获得高编码容量和低维可解缠的潜在空间。

Method: 1) 将VAE训练建模为软约束优化问题；2) 对单个潜在变量施加熵约束（EC），证明潜在编码的熵是其与数据生成因子互信息的上界；3) 提出权重过滤方法，利用软约束的松弛性在下游训练中剪枝低熵维度。

Result: dSprites上：潜在变量激活分数提升43-62%，FactorVAE得分达0.891（对比β-VAE变体最高0.847），重构误差降低38%。MNIST上：下游分类器的潜在维度从10降至2，准确率保持90%以上，收敛速度提升37%。

Conclusion: 熵约束有效平衡了编码容量与解缠性，且低熵离散因子倾向于合并到单个潜在变量中，高熵连续因子则分布到多个变量上，揭示了不同性质因子的编码规律。

Abstract: The usefulness of a variational autoencoder (VAE) depends on two properties of its latent space that are hard to obtain together: high encoding capacity in the individual latent variables, and a low-dimensional, disentangled organization of those variables. Weakening the Kullback-Leibler regularization raises capacity but degrades disentanglement, while strengthening it prunes latent variables away entirely. We formulate VAE training as a soft-constrained optimization problem that addresses both. First, we impose an entropy-based constraint (EC) on individual latent variables, showing that the entropy of a latent code upper-bounds the mutual information it carries about the generative factors of the data. Second, we propose a weight-filter method that exploits the slack of the soft constraint to prune low-entropy dimensions during downstream training. On dSprites, the EC raises the aggregate latent-variable activation score by 43-62% over a vanilla VAE, attains the highest FactorVAE score among the \b{eta} \b{eta}-VAE variants (0.891 vs 0.847), and lowers reconstruction error by up to 38%. On MNIST, the weight filter reduces the latent dimensionality supplied to a downstream classifier from ten to two while holding accuracy above 90%, converging in 37% fewer epochs than the same procedure without the EC. We also find that low-entropy discrete factors tend to merge into a single latent variable, whereas high-entropy continuous factors are distributed across several.

</details>


### [105] [On the post-hoc Evaluation of PDE Discovery: A Multifaceted Challenge of Scientific Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23753)
*Baptiste Mathevon,Farah Cherfaoui,Amaury Habrard,Marc Sebban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对偏微分方程发现中的评估指标进行了系统分类，分析了现有指标的优缺点，并提出了标准化评估的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE发现后的评估是多方面的（需同时考虑预测精度、物理一致性、可解释性和分布外泛化能力），现有评估指标只部分解决了整体问题，可能导致对物理理论有效性的过度解读。缺乏统一的评估方法论。

Method: 基于对机器学习、数值分析、信息论和符号回归等领域文献的综述，提出了第一个PDE评估指标分类体系，深入讨论了各类指标的优缺点。

Result: 建立了PDE评估指标的首次分类学，指出了现有方法的局限性，并提供了标准化评估的推荐实践。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为设计PDE发现算法的ML专家以及在实际应用中发现和验证科学规律的用户提供指导，并指出了该领域未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) discovery aims to identify from data the governing law of a physical system. Constituting a cornerstone of scientific advancement, it has become during the past decade a major line of research in the rapidly evolving field of Physics-informed Machine Learning (PiML). Among the remaining open problems to address in this domain, the post-hoc evaluation of discovered PDEs raises the particular difficulty of being multifaceted. Indeed, it requires jointly considering predictive accuracy, physical consistency, interpretability, and out-of-distribution generalization capacity. Given that some of these properties are conflicting, it is worth noting that the wide range of existing evaluation metrics only partially address the overall problem, potentially leading to overly interpreted conclusions about the validity of a presumed new physical theory. From an abundant literature spanning machine learning, numerical analysis, information theory or symbolic regression, we propose, to our knowledge, the first taxonomy of PDE evaluation metrics, and discuss their advantages and limitations in depth. Based on the observation that evaluation is often achieved on a case-by-case basis and that a universally accepted methodology remains elusive, we further provide recommendations with the aim of promoting standardized and reliable practices, before sketching promising future lines of research in this field. We argue that this paper is intended both for ML experts who design new PDE discovery algorithms and for users of these methods aiming, in real applications, to discover and validate well-founded scientific laws.

</details>


### [106] [WISERouter: LLM Routing with Workload Budget Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23765)
*Yifei Li,Zihui Gao,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WISERouter框架，将LLM路由问题建模为带约束的上下文多臂老虎机问题，支持离线/在线学习，在RouterBench和SWE-Bench上表现优于现有方法，且在线版本具有O(√T)的亚线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
