Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在多个维度（领域、能力、任务难度、交互场景）上的进展呈现碎片化状态，缺乏统一的全场景扩展框架来协调智能体的定义、训练、评估和改进流程。

Method: 提出AgentOmnia框架，包含：(1) 可扩展的领域×能力×原子难度分类法，用于任务空间定义和细粒度诊断；(2) 双向环境-任务合成与工具依赖、程序结构化、求解器三种数据管道，构建了5,018个状态化环境、255,375个工具和52,361个任务；(3) 基于监督微调、在线智能体强化学习和回滚课程的后训练流程；(4) 评估失败转化为PRD用于自我进化的闭环机制。

Result: 在OmniaBench挑战集上通过率从9.16%提升至37.11%；四个基准宏平均从22.86%提升至41.69%；超越Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507在所有四个基准上的表现；在76/90一级领域上均有提升，覆盖三个应用类别、十个能力维度和八个难度因子。

Conclusion: AgentOmnia展现了在统一协议下实现全场景智能体扩展的可行性，通过PRD引导的自我进化机制初步验证了闭环改进的潜力，为更大规模工业和学术应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents have advanced rapidly, yet progress remains fragmented across domains, capabilities, task difficulty, and interaction settings. We frame this as full-scenario agentic scaling and present AgentOmnia, a framework coordinating task-space definition, data synthesis, post-training, evaluation, and improvement across To-Consumer (ToC), To-Business (ToB), and To-Employee (ToE) applications. An extensible Domain x Capability x Atomic Difficulty taxonomy aligns these stages and enables fine-grained diagnosis with OmniaBench. AgentOmnia combines bidirectional environment-task synthesis with tool-dependency, program-structured, and solver-based pipelines, constructing 5,018 stateful environments with 255,375 tools and 52,361 tasks. Programs, solvers, and verifiers provide correctness signals, while supervised fine-tuning, online agentic reinforcement learning, and a rollback curriculum support post-training. Evaluation failures translate into Product Requirement Documents (PRDs) for targeted self-evolution. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507, AgentOmnia raises the pass rate on the OmniaBench challenging subset from 9.16% to 37.11% and the macro-average across OmniaBench, $τ^2$-Bench, DeepPlanning, and VitaBench from 22.86% to 41.69%. Under a unified protocol,it leads the evaluated agentic post-trained baselines on OmniaBench and retains the highest four-benchmark macro-average. It also surpasses Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 on all four benchmarks and exceeds Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the macro-average. Gains span three application splits, ten capability dimensions, eight atomic-difficulty factors, and 76 of 90 level-1 domains, indicating broad rather than category-specific improvement. A one-round study provides initial evidence for PRD-guided self-evolution, motivating validation at larger scales and in industrial settings.

</details>


### [285] [CachedSearch: Training-Free Cached Exploration for Test-Time Search in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23159)
*Shreshth Saini,Neil Birkbeck,Yilin Wang,Balu Adsumilli,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CachedSearch方法，在视频扩散模型的测试时搜索中，通过缓存加速候选生成+全量重算胜出者，以63%成本捕获94.7%的收益，且训练无关、验证器无关，适用于多种模型家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时搜索让小型视频扩散模型媲美大型模型，但计算成本高2-10倍。所有候选都被完整去噪，但大多数被丢弃，造成大量浪费。需要一种训练无关的方法来降低搜索成本而不损害候选排名质量。

Method: CachedSearch方法：1) 使用自适应缓存包装器对每个候选进行加速生成（约2倍加速）；2) 对缓存胜出者进行全量重算；3) 验证缓存是否破坏验证器排名（发现错误集中在近并列候选，具有自限性）；4) 支持中轨迹剪枝进一步加速。

Result: Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B上，缓存后的中位Spearman秩相关0.905，72% top-1一致。N=8时，CachedSearch以63%成本捕获94.7%的best-of-N收益。匹配预算时，搜索宽度翻倍，收益增加38%。从1.3B到14B，跨6个模型和4个家族（Wan, LTX, CogVideoX, Hunyuan）均有效。中轨迹剪枝实现3.11x加速，88.6%捕获率。

Conclusion: CachedSearch是训练无关、验证器无关、与搜索算法正交的即插即用乘数，可有效扩展测试时计算。保真度追踪架构而非参数数量，跨模型家族仅需重新校准一个参数。

Abstract: Test-time search lets small video diffusion models rival larger ones, but costs 2-10x more. All candidates are fully denoised, although most are discarded. Training-free caching makes each rollout 2-3x faster at near-lossless quality. Composition is safe only if lossy caching preserves verifier rankings. We present the first study of whether caching corrupts candidate ranking in video test-time search. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B with an adaptive caching wrapper (~2x per-candidate speedup), ImageReward scores seed-matched cached and full rollouts. Median per-prompt Spearman rank correlation is 0.905, with 72% top-1 agreement on the VBench suite. VBench-2.0 replicates this result on a harder suite. Recomputing the cached winner at full compute retains 90-94% of the full-search gain. Errors cluster among near-tied candidates, making corruption self-limiting. This finding leads to CachedSearch. It explores every candidate with aggressive caching, then re-generates only the winner at full compute. At N=8, it captures 94.7% of best-of-N's gain at 63% of the cost. Capture rises with width. At matched budget, it searches twice as wide for 38% more gain. The result holds from 1.3B-14B across six models and four families: Wan, LTX, CogVideoX, and Hunyuan. Wan2.1-14B matches the 1.3B model's fidelity. Mid-trajectory pruning multiplies the exploration saving to 3.11x at 88.6% capture. Ports to other model families require recalibrating a single parameter, showing that fidelity tracks architecture rather than parameter count. CachedSearch is training-free, verifier-agnostic, and orthogonal to the search algorithm, making it a plug-in multiplier for test-time scaling.

</details>


### [286] [An Ontology for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23219)
*Daniel Hernández,Jong Hyun Jung,Yuji Ikeda,Yongliang Ou,Pranav Kumar,Tom Schächtel,Wenchuan Liu,Xin Li,Xi Zhang,Xiang Xu,Lifang Zhu,Fritz Körmann,Steffen Staab,Blazej Grabowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MLIPs本体论，一个用于描述机器学习原子间势方法、超参数、训练数据集和基准测试的OWL 2 DL本体论，包含方法、训练数据和基准测试三个模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP领域缺乏统一的元数据标准来系统比较、复现和构建相关研究，相关元数据分散在论文、脚本和临时文件格式中。

Method: 构建了OWL 2 DL本体论，包含三个模块（方法、训练数据、基准测试），连接现有材料科学和机器学习本体论，声明了27个形式化公理以确保数据完整性和一致性，并通过属性链将训练模型与其方法和训练数据关联。

Result: 通过基于力矩张量势的示例进行展示，并在20篇论文种子知识图谱上执行能力问题测试、OWL推理，以及与现有本体论的比较来评估。

Conclusion: MLIPs本体论能够有效捕获MLIP方法、超参数、训练数据集和基准测试的元数据，促进研究的可比较性、可复现性和可构建性。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) approximate quantum-mechanical energies and forces---conventionally computed by density functional theory (DFT) or wave-function methods---at a fraction of the cost. The field encompasses a growing ecosystem of algorithms, training datasets, hyperparameters, and target materials, yet the metadata needed to systematically compare, reproduce, and build upon MLIP studies remains scattered across papers, scripts, and ad-hoc file formats. We present the MLIPs ontology, an OWL 2 DL ontology that captures the concepts needed to describe MLIP methods, their hyperparameters, training datasets with DFT provenance, and published benchmarks. The ontology is organized into three modules---Method, Training Data, and Benchmark---and connects existing ontologies in materials science (MDO, CMSO/ASMO) and machine learning (ML-Schema), complementing dataset-side schemas such as Croissant. It declares 27 formal axioms enforcing data completeness and consistency, including property chains that link trained models to their methods and training data. We demonstrate the ontology through a running example based on Moment Tensor Potentials and evaluate it through competency-question execution on a 20-paper seeded knowledge graph, OWL reasoning, and comparison with existing ontologies.

</details>


### [287] [Characterisation of Density-based FM generation methods in the context of Information Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23243)
*Yanhao Huang,Christian Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于模糊密度的传统模糊测度参数化方法通常无法唯一确定离散模糊测度，但可以唯一确定区间值模糊测度。通过引入额外信息（如特定模糊积分和数据集），可获得更精确的区间值模糊测度，并能以给定置信水平计算其包含"理想"数值模糊测度的置信区间。实验表明，基于此模糊测度的Choquet模糊积分输出也可视为"理想"信息融合结果的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊积分聚合中，模糊测度的参数化是关键挑战。现有Sugeno-λ和可分解模糊测度等方法从密度（个体源权重）外推参数化，但该信息通常不足以唯一确定离散模糊测度，需要探索更完善的确定方法。

Method: (1) 理论分析证明仅基于密度信息无法唯一确定离散FM，但可唯一确定区间值FM；(2) 引入额外信息（特定FI和数据集）以获得更具体的区间值FM；(3) 提出统计方法计算区间FM包含"理想"数值FM的似然，生成给定置信水平下的置信区间；(4) 通过实验验证Choquet FI输出可作为"理想"融合结果的置信区间。

Result: (1) 证明了基于密度信息只能唯一确定区间值FM而非确定值；(2) 额外信息可缩小区间值FM的范围；(3) 可计算区间FM包含理想FM的置信区间；(4) 实验表明Choquet FI输出本身也可视为理想融合结果的置信区间。

Conclusion: 传统基于密度的FM参数化方法的信息不足以唯一确定离散FM，但区间值FM可以唯一确定。该框架提供了在给定置信水平下表征FI融合结果置信区间的新方法，为未来研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Fuzzy Integral (FI) based aggregation provides a powerful mechanism for nuanced aggregation, for example, in ensemble approaches or decision-level fusion more generally. The main challenge of this approach is the appropriate parametrization of the Fuzzy Measure (FM), which captures the worths of the individual components--and their combinations--which are being fused. Here, widely used approaches including the Sugeno-$λ$ and Decomposable FMs, parametrize the FM by extrapolating from the densities, i.e. the weights associated with individual sources, while respecting the FM's monotonicity constraint. This paper articulates that this information is, in general, insufficient to uniquely identify a discrete FM; but shows how an interval-valued FM can indeed be determined uniquely. We proceed to show how the incorporation of additional information beyond the above, such as the choice of a specific FI and a dataset, then allows for obtaining even more specific interval-valued FMs. In practice, establishing the quality of an empirically determined FM is not trivial. To help address this, we show how the likelihood with which a resulting interval FM encompasses the `ideal', i.e. the commonly intangible, best, or ground-truth numeric FM, can be determined, producing a confidence interval at a given confidence level. Finally, based on a series of experiments, we demonstrate empirically that the Choquet FI output based on this FM can also be regarded as the confidence interval for the `ideal' information fusion result, providing a novel means to characterize FI fusion outcomes a priori and charting a pathway for future research.

</details>


### [288] [CAPT: A Multi-task Continuous Autoregressive Transformer enabling Cross-dataset and Cross-species Transfer for Calcium Population Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23258)
*Xinhong Xu,Yimeng Zhang,Yuanlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPT（连续自回归群体Transformer），一种直接对钙成像群体神经活动进行连续自回归建模的基础模型。通过在大规模小鼠数据集上预训练，并在小鼠、斑马鱼和线虫等跨物种数据集上冻结骨干网络、仅微调适配模块，CAPT在神经活动预测和行为解码任务上均超越专用和通用基线。多模态分析表明其嵌入空间捕捉了跨数据集的共享功能结构和细胞身份信息，证实了连续自回归建模是构建跨数据集、跨范式、跨物种的神经基础模型的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模钙成像数据为构建神经群体动力学的基座模型提供了机会，但现有方法多为特定任务设计并在单一数据集上评估，不清楚学习到的表示是否可跨数据集、实验范式和物种泛化。核心问题是：能否用一个模型在多个钙成像记录上预训练后，泛化到全新的数据上。

Method: 1) 连续补丁分词策略：直接对连续钙成像信号进行分块token化，无需离散化或事件检测；2) 自回归预训练：以因果自回归方式预测下一时间步的钙信号token；3) 大规模预训练后冻结骨干，仅更新适配模块（如线性探针或轻量解码器）迁移到下游任务（神经活动预测和行为解码）。

Result: 1) 跨数据集/跨物种迁移：在独立的小鼠、斑马鱼幼虫和线虫数据集上，CAPT的冻结骨干在神经活动预测和行为解码中一致优于专用和通用基线；2) 利用线虫NeuroPAL注释的多模态分析表明，CAPT嵌入形成了跨数据集的共享功能空间，并捕捉了与解剖学细胞身份相关的结构。

Conclusion: 连续自回归建模为构建跨数据集、跨实验范式、跨物种的通用神经基座模型提供了一条简单可行的路径，表明钙成像领域有望发展出类似自然语言和视觉领域的基础模型。

Abstract: Large-scale calcium imaging has created an opportunity to build foundation-style models for neural population dynamics, but a central question remains unresolved: \textbf{whether a model pretrained on one collection of recordings can generalize to new datasets, experimental paradigms, and even species.} Existing approaches are often designed for specific tasks and evaluated on a single dataset, making it unclear whether their learned representations are reusable for new calcium trace datasets. To tackle this gap, we present \textbf{CAPT}, a \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{A}utoregressive \textbf{P}opulation \textbf{T}ransformer for calcium population dynamics. CAPT models continuous calcium traces directly through a continuous patch tokenization strategy and is trained autoregressively, enabling end-to-end pretraining and adaptation to diverse downstream tasks. We first pretrain CAPT on a large-scale mouse calcium imaging dataset and evaluate its transferability across independent mouse, larval zebrafish, and \textit{C. elegans} datasets collected by different laboratories. In these transfer settings, the pretrained backbone is frozen and only adaptation modules are updated. Across neural population forecasting and behavior decoding tasks, CAPT consistently outperforms specialized and general-purpose baselines. Alongside predictive performance, multimodal analyses using NeuroPAL annotations in \textit{C. elegans} datasets show that CAPT embeddings form a shared functional space across datasets and capture anatomical cell-identity-related structure. These results suggest that the continuous autoregressive modeling opens up possibilities for a simple route towards general-purpose neural foundation models for calcium imaging, which can generalize across datasets, experimental paradigms, and species.

</details>


### [289] [SeekJudge: A Practical Reward Framework for Reinforcement Learning in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23263)
*Yang Wan,Zhenhao Zhang,Jierui Wang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SeekJudge框架，通过四个角色专业化智能体（Condense、Ground、Seek、Analyze）和种子校准蒸馏管道训练共享9B模型，首次实现基于模型的奖励在在线RL中匹配或超越原生规则监督，并提供步骤级判断、低成本、长轨迹支持等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的评估方法难以对齐人类意图，且在应用更新或内容漂移时失效；现有基于模型的判断器虽试图解决这些问题，但与规则评估相比仍存在性能差距。因此需要一种更准确、可扩展且实用的模型驱动评估方法。

Method: SeekJudge框架包含四个角色专业化智能体：Condense（压缩轨迹）、Ground（锚定指令）、Seek（搜索证据）和Analyze（分析判定），通过Seek-Analyze循环得出裁决。采用种子校准蒸馏管道训练单个9B模型作为所有四个智能体的共享骨干。

Result: 在下游RL测试目标的成功率上，SeekJudge是首个在在线RL中匹配或超越原生规则监督的实用模型奖励。此外，它提供步骤级判断，运行成本远低于闭源大模型，且保持小上下文窗口以支持更长轨迹。

Conclusion: SeekJudge使基于模型的奖励成为CUA强化学习中规则监督的实用即插即用替代方案。此外还提出了奖励服务器的通用架构改进，加速RL中的判断过程。

Abstract: Deciding whether a trajectory actually fulfills its instruction governs how we measure computer-use agents on long-horizon graphical-user-interface tasks and how we train them with reinforcement learning. This judgment has long relied on rule-based evaluation, which struggles to align with human intention and goes stale when an app updates or its online content drifts. Existing model-based judges attempt to address these problems but still leave a performance gap to the rule-based evaluation. We propose the \textbf{SeekJudge} framework, in which four role-specialized agents, a Condense, a Ground, a Seek and an Analyze agent, reach a verdict through a Seek--Analyze loop over the trajectory. A seed-calibrated distillation pipeline trains one specialized $9$B model to serve as the shared backbone for all four agents. Measured by downstream success rate on held-out RL test goals, SeekJudge is the first practical model-based reward to match or surpass native rule-based supervision in online RL. Beyond accuracy, SeekJudge provides step-level judgments, runs far cheaper than a closed-source large model, and keeps a small per-call context that scales to much longer trajectories. We further contribute a general architectural improvement to the reward server that speeds up judging in RL. Together these make model-based reward a practical drop-in for rule-based supervision in CUA reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [290] [TopoFE: topology-aware LLM-guided Automated Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23286)
*Sha Li,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TOPOFE是一个拓扑感知的多岛屿进化框架，利用LLM进行自动特征工程，通过家族特化探索、自适应提示记忆和拓扑引导知识迁移，在29个表格数据集上优于现有方法，且发现的特征程序更具多样性和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的AutoFE方法受限于无状态生成和同质化搜索：特征提案由静态提示生成，无法积累搜索经验；单种群探索快速收敛到主导变换模式，难以发现跨变换家族的互补特征组合。

Method: 提出TOPOFE框架，结合(1)家族特化探索，(2)自适应提示记忆，以及(3)拓扑引导的知识迁移，在多个岛屿上并行进化特征程序，实现多样化和组合式的特征发现。

Result: 在29个公开表格数据集上，TOPOFE在分类和回归任务中均一致优于现有SOTA AutoFE方法，且发现的特征程序具有更高的多样性和可迁移性，能泛化到多个下游预测器和LLM骨干网络。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的多岛屿进化框架有效克服了LLM在AutoFE中的无状态和同质化局限，能够发现更优、更多样且可迁移的特征程序。

Abstract: Automatic feature engineering (AutoFE) for tabular learning can be naturally formulated as a program synthesis problem, where the objective is to discover predictive feature transformations from an exponentially large search space. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have expanded the expressiveness of AutoFE by enabling feature program generation beyond predefined operator libraries. However, existing LLM-based approaches remain fundamentally limited by stateless generation and homogeneous search: feature proposals are produced from static prompts without accumulating search experience, while single-population exploration quickly converges to dominant transformation patterns and rarely discovers complementary feature compositions across transformation families. We propose TOPOFE, a topology-aware multi-island evolutionary framework for LLM-guided feature engineering. TOPOFE combines family-specialized exploration, adaptive prompt memory, and topology-guided knowledge transfer to efficiently discover diverse and compositional feature programs. Experiments on 29 public tabular datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art AutoFE methods across classification and regression tasks. Beyond predictive performance, TOPOFE discovers more diverse and transferable feature programs that generalize across multiple downstream predictors and LLM backbones.

</details>


### [291] [RareLens: Towards End-to-End Rare Disease Care via Aligning Divergent Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23290)
*Xi Chen,Hongru Zhou,Shiyu Feng,Hanyu Zhou,Huahui Yi,Rongsheng Wang,Tiancheng He,Kun Wang,Pingping Liu,Qiankun Li,Sicheng Lin,Huiying Ou,Xiaohong Zheng,Tianying Zang,Zhuohang Wu,Leheng Jiang,Kexin Cao,Wenhan Zhang,ChengYi Li,Zhiyang Wang,Songlin Li,Benyou Wang,Ningbei Yin,Shaoting Zhang,Weili Fu,Jian Li,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RareLens是一个利用异构大语言模型间的分歧推理进行对齐与校准的系统，覆盖罕见病全程（筛查、诊断、治疗、预后）。在15.7万例真实数据上，其各阶段表现均超越GPT-5、DeepSeek-R1等前沿模型，筛查AUC达0.917，诊断和治疗的Top-1准确率分别为65.5%和89.8%。外部研究显示，自主RareLens及辅助医生的表现均显著优于未受辅助的医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病误诊率高（>70%），早期症状非特异，相关专业知识稀缺且分布不均。现有AI系统仅覆盖孤立环节（主要为诊断），依赖下游检查结果，并将模型间的变异性视为需要消除的噪声，而非可利用的信息。

Method: 构建RareLens，包含四个协调模块（初诊筛查、诊断、治疗规划、预后）。核心思路是利用异构大语言模型对同一病例产生的分歧但互补的推理，通过对齐与校准将其整合为单一收敛的可行动决策。

Result: 在RareBench（157,525例，涵盖33个Orphanet类别和7000+疾病）上，RareLens在每一阶段均超越GPT-5、DeepSeek-R1、Claude-3.7-Sonnet、Gemini-2.5-Pro。筛查AUC为0.917，诊断Top-1准确率65.5%，治疗Top-1准确率89.8%。外部研究（1,287例，23名医生）中，自主RareLens和RareLens辅助的医生均显著优于未辅助医生。

Conclusion: 对齐多样化模型的推理分歧（而非单纯扩缩单一模型）为高不确定性临床决策提供了一种可泛化的策略。

Abstract: Rare diseases collectively affect an estimated 3.5% to 5.9% of the population, yet more than 70% of patients are misdiagnosed and many endure years of evaluation before a diagnosis is reached, because early presentations are nonspecific and relevant expertise is scarce and unevenly distributed. Artificial intelligence could provide support, but existing systems address isolated stages of care, overwhelmingly diagnosis. They typically depend on the results of downstream investigations, and they treat the variability between models as noise to be eliminated. Here we present RareLens, a system that supports clinical decision-making across the entire rare disease trajectory by exploiting this variability. When heterogeneous large language models evaluate the same case, they generate divergent but complementary reasoning, which RareLens aligns and calibrates into a single convergent, actionable decision at each stage. Four coordinated modules perform primary-visit risk screening, diagnosis, treatment planning and prognosis. Developed and evaluated on RareBench, a real-world dataset of 157,525 cases spanning all 33 Orphanet categories and more than 7,000 conditions, RareLens outperformed every frontier model tested, including GPT-5, DeepSeek-R1, Claude-3.7-Sonnet and Gemini-2.5-Pro, at each stage. It achieved an area under the curve of 0.917 for screening and top-1 accuracies of 65.5% and 89.8% for diagnosis and treatment. In an external study spanning 1,287 cases and 23 physicians, autonomous RareLens and physicians assisted by RareLens both substantially outperformed unaided physicians. These findings indicate that aligning divergent model reasoning, rather than scaling a single model, offers a generalizable strategy for high-uncertainty clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [292] [Ordered Network Analysis of Epistemic Emotions during Collaborative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23317)
*Sifatul Anindho,Videep Venkatesha,Jaclyn Ocumpaugh,Nathaniel Blanchard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用有序网络分析(ONA)研究协作问题解决中情感状态（如困惑、挫败感）的持续与转换模式，发现不同报告方法（自我捕获vs探针捕获）和不同速度组（快组vs慢组）在情感状态的有序结构上存在显著差异，并识别出一个由好奇心、乐观和困惑构成的稳定认知核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解协作问题解决中情感状态的动态变化对认识认知情感动力学至关重要，但情感状态的准确识别仍具挑战性，缺乏金标准。

Method: 通过回顾性提示回忆收集协作任务中的情感状态数据，采用有序网络分析(ONA)比较不同报告方法（自我捕获/探针捕获）和不同任务速度组的情感状态有序结构差异。

Result: ONA揭示了描述性统计无法显示的持续与转换模式差异；发现好奇心、乐观和困惑构成的稳定认知核心；不同报告方法强调不同的状态间连接；快慢组在困惑和脱离状态的角色上存在显著差异，尤其体现在与冲突的关系上。

Conclusion: ONA能有效揭示协作中情感状态的动态结构差异，其结果对开发支持协作问题解决的人工智能系统具有重要启示。

Abstract: Investigating how affective states such as confusion and frustration persist and transition during co-situated collaborative problem solving (CPS) is important for understanding the dynamics of epistemic emotions. However, the accurate identification of affective states remain challenging as there is no gold-standard truth in this space. Here, we analyze affective states collected through retrospective cued-recall during an in-person CPS task. Using ordered network analysis (ONA), we examine (1) the overall ordered structure of affective states and how this structure differs across self-caught and probe-caught reporting methods, and (2) what aspects of this ordered structure are emphasized differently in slower and faster groups. We find that ONA reveals differences in persistence and transition patterns that are not apparent from descriptive summaries alone. In particular, we observe a stable epistemic core linking curiosity, optimism, and confusion, with different reporting methods emphasizing different connections among states. An analysis between faster and slower groups show that roles of confusion and disengagement also shift significantly during collaboration, particularly in their relationship to conflict. We interpret our findings in the context of collaboration and discuss their implications in developing AI systems that support CPS.

</details>


### [293] [ESF-Bench: Benchmarking Challenging Slot-Filling Scenarios for Real-World Enterprise Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23326)
*Toby Liang,Gopal Sarda,Sagar Davasam,Vikas Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ESF-Bench，一个涵盖8个领域、810个多轮样本和6530个槽位的企业级槽填充基准测试，基于实际部署中57个最具挑战性的场景构建。实验表明，当前最先进的GPT-OSS-120b模型仅能成功提取20.7%的样本槽位，揭示了现有LLM在企业级槽填充任务中的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在企业实际部署中面临复杂系统约束和意外用户行为等独特挑战，现有基准无法充分反映这些真实场景中的槽填充难题，亟需一个针对企业级应用的高难度基准来暴露当前模型的不足。

Method: 基于企业实际部署中观察到的57种最具挑战性的槽填充场景构建分类体系，并据此创建了包含8个领域、810个多轮样本和6530个槽位的ESF-Bench基准数据集。

Result: 在ESF-Bench上，当前最先进的GPT-OSS-120b模型仅能成功提取20.7%的样本槽位，表明现有LLM在企业级槽填充任务中存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在企业级槽填充任务中表现不足，ESF-Bench为该领域研究提供了具有挑战性的基准，作者已公开数据集、分类体系和评估代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: The rapid rise of large language models (LLMs) has driven transformative adoption across enterprises. However, deploying these models in real-world settings presents unique challenges due to complex system constraints and unexpected user behaviors. Among these applications, slot filling is essential for converting unstructured input into structured, actionable data. In this work, we introduce ESF-Bench, a challenging Enterprise Slot Filling benchmark consisting of 810 multi-turn samples and 6530 slots over 8 unique domains. Curated using a taxonomy of the 57 most challenging slot-filling scenarios observed during real-world enterprise deployments, ESF-Bench exposes notable limitations in current state-of-the-art LLMs, with GPT-OSS-120b low successfully extracting slots for only 20.7% of benchmark samples. To support continued research in this area, we publicly release the benchmark dataset, taxonomy, and accompanying evaluation code on GitHub.

</details>


### [294] [Confidently Wrong: Exception Chain Collapse in Frontier LLM Rule Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23386)
*Paul Simpson,John Kozak,Lisa Doake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在嵌套条件规则下存在"异常链崩塌"故障，并提出了一种神经符号架构Aethis Eligibility Module，将LLM用于规则编写、SMT求解器用于确定性执行，从而将不确定性从推理边界转移到可审计的规范边界。实验表明该架构在多个领域和基准上显著优于前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型在嵌套条件规则（如"A适用，除非B，除非C覆盖B"）的资格评估任务中存在系统性故障——"异常链崩塌"。更严重的是，模型精度会在无版本更新的情况下悄然变化（如GPT-5.4从96.6%升至100%），这对受监管的工作流构成合规风险，因为决策边界的不确定性是静默且不可控的。

Method: 提出Aethis Eligibility Module，一种神经符号架构：LLM负责从权威来源起草规则，SMT（可满足性模理论）求解器则基于这些规则确定性执行推理。该架构将LLM限制在规则创作阶段（可审计），而将执行阶段交给形式化方法，从而隔离模型漂移、推理努力默认值和提示格式变化的影响。

Result: 三组证据：（1）225个场景的受控基准验证了故障模式及其漂移；（2）20个对抗性场景中，引擎得20/20，仅1/4的前沿配置（GPT-5.4低推理努力）同样满分，其余3个前沿模型（包括Anthropic最强模型）均失败；（3）LegalBench 9个任务949例外部验证中，引擎显著优于所有前沿模型（McNemar p≤0.003），在多项复合任务上领先Anthropic模型最高达+41个百分点。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将不确定性从静默的推理边界重新定位到可设计、可审计的规范边界。所有场景、规则编码和结果均已公开可复现。

Abstract: We document a failure class in frontier large language models -- exception chain collapse -- observed in eligibility evaluation under nested conditional rules of the form "A is required UNLESS B applies, UNLESS C overrides B". The failure reproduces at first observation, but its empirical surface is unstable: between March and April 2026 several failure cells closed silently under the same model alias, with no version bump (GPT-5.4 on construction insurance moved from 96.6% to 100%, same prompt and harness). For regulated workflows, frontier-model accuracy is a moving compliance boundary that shifts without notice. We present the Aethis Eligibility Module, a neuro-symbolic architecture in which LLMs author rules from authoritative sources and an SMT-based layer executes them deterministically, consistent with the authored specification regardless of model drift, reasoning-effort defaults, or prompt format. Three evidence bases: (i) a controlled benchmark of 225 scenarios across four regulatory domains documents the pattern and, in replication, the drift that partially closed it; (ii) a 20-scenario adversarial extension on construction insurance, where the engine scores 20/20, as does one of four frontier configurations (GPT-5.4 at low reasoning effort), while the other three, including Anthropic's strongest model at evaluation time, fail the same coverage-gap edge case; (iii) external validation on nine peer-reviewed LegalBench tasks, 949 held-out cases, where the engine is significantly more accurate than all three frontier models (combined McNemar's p <= 0.003), with margins up to +41 points on the curated multi-prong tasks against the Anthropic models. The contribution is to relocate uncertainty from the inference boundary, where it is silent, to the specification boundary, where it is deliberate and audited. All scenarios, rule encodings, and results are public and reproducible.

</details>


### [295] [Key-Interval A*: Accelerating Grid Pathfinding via Structural Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23393)
*Taiquan Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KIA*是一种针对4连通网格寻径的最优算法，通过轻量预处理构建区间级抽象图，在保持最优性的同时显著加快搜索速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确4连通网格寻径方法要么保留细粒度搜索状态导致搜索开销大，要么需要大量预处理。本文旨在设计一种既能减少在线搜索又无需大量预处理的轻量级最优寻径算法。

Method: KIA*通过预处理将自由空间表示为最大连续通行单元区间，提取关键区间（捕捉结构边界变化）并通过连续非关键区域连接，构建关键区间图，然后在该图上执行A*搜索，最后从区间链构造性地重建网格路径（无需逐格局部搜索）。

Result: 在8个标准基准测试组中的7个上取得最快运行时间，在结构化地图和游戏地图上提升最大，且保持了精确最短路径长度。

Conclusion: KIA*被证明在4连通网格上具有完备性和最优性，通过区间级抽象有效平衡了预处理开销与在线搜索效率。

Abstract: Existing exact methods for 4-connected grid pathfinding reduce online search, but often either retain fine-grained search states or require substantial preprocessing. This paper presents Key-Interval A* (KIA*), an optimal pathfinding algorithm that uses lightweight preprocessing to construct and search over a compact interval-level abstraction of free space. KIA* represents free space using intervals: maximal contiguous runs of traversable cells. It extracts key intervals that capture structural boundary changes and connects them through contiguous non-key regions. KIA* then performs A*-style search on the resulting key-interval graph and constructively reconstructs grid paths from interval chains, without cell-level local search. We prove the completeness and optimality of KIA* on 4-connected grids. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that KIA* preserves exact shortest-path lengths and achieves the fastest runtime on seven of eight benchmark groups, with the largest gains on structured and game maps.

</details>


### [296] [Inference-Time Consensus for Mitigating Hidden Behaviors from LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23394)
*Adhyyan Narang,Artin Tajdini,Claire Zhang,Jamie Morgenstern*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过冗余性（多源数据共识解码）防御语言模型后门攻击，在解码时聚合多个参考模型的分布，使用最小概率阈值和基座相对变体两种共识解码器，有效抑制特定源的恶意行为同时保留共享的良性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准防御（数据过滤、混合无害数据、正则化）只能减弱后门攻击和隐藏偏好传播的效果，无法完全消除，需要更强的鲁棒性方法。

Method: 收集多个不同来源的数据集，每个来源微调一个独立的参考模型；解码时聚合各模型的下一个token分布，提出两种共识解码器：(1) 逐token最小值——将每个token的概率上限设为所有源中最低值；(2) 基座相对变体——当各源对某token的移动方向不一致时回退到基座模型概率。进一步放松精确一致要求，允许部分源支持和不同表面表达。

Result: 在受控投毒任务、潜意识学习和突现错位对齐等场景中，共识解码有效抑制了特定源的恶意行为，同时保留了共享的良性行为；在联合训练和权重平均仍保留不良行为的案例中，该方法依然有效。

Conclusion: 共识解码通过多源冗余和聚合机制，能在不牺牲良性性能的前提下防御后门攻击和隐藏偏好传播，是一种比现有标准防御更有效的鲁棒性策略。

Abstract: Recent work shows that fine-tuning language models on even a small amount of poisoned data can install targeted misbehavior, and ostensibly benign data can transmit hidden preferences that generalize broadly. Standard defenses, such as data filtering, mixing in harmless data, and regularization, attenuate these effects but do not eliminate them. We instead pursue robustness through redundancy: collecting multiple datasets from different sources and only learning what is common between them. Thus, if only a subset of sources are malicious, the misbehavior will be blocked. In order to implement this defense strategy, we fine-tune a separate reference model on each source's dataset and aggregate their next-token distributions at decoding time. We introduce two consensus decoders: a token-wise minimum, which caps each token at the lowest probability any source assigns, and a base-relative variant, which reverts to the base probability on any token the sources move in opposing directions. We further relax exact agreement to tolerate partial support across sources and different surface expressions of the same intention. Across controlled poisoning tasks, subliminal learning, and emergent misalignment, consensus decoding suppresses source-specific misbehavior while preserving shared desirable behavior, including cases where union training and weight averaging retain the unwanted behavior.

</details>


### [297] [NeurGO: Learning to Generate Elite Candidates for Meta-Black-Box Expensive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23408)
*Jintao He,Huixiang Zhen,Wenyin Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeurGO提出了一种基于生成式元黑箱优化（MetaBBO）框架，利用注意力编码器捕获种群搜索趋势，并以此条件生成高质量候选解，通过质量-多样性损失维持搜索性能，在CEC 2008和COCO BBOB测试集上实现了更优的优化效果和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昂贵的黑箱优化中，传统进化算法和MetaBBO方法在候选选择上浪费了大量评估预算于劣质解；代理模型辅助方法虽旨在减少评估，但构建精确全局模型困难且模型偏差易导致局部最优。因此需要一种能直接生成精英候选解、避免浪费评估预算的新方法。

Method: 提出NeurGO框架：1）采用基于注意力机制的编码器捕获种群级搜索趋势；2）解码器以该表示为条件直接生成高质量候选解，避免评估大规模子代池；3）设计质量-多样性损失函数，在搜索过程中同时维持解的质量和种群多样性。

Result: 在CEC 2008和COCO BBOB测试集上的广泛基准测试表明，在相同评估预算下，NeurGO实现了更优的优化性能并展现出更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: NeurGO通过生成式方法直接合成精英候选解，有效克服了传统MetaBBO和代理模型辅助方法的局限性，为昂贵黑箱优化提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Expensive black-box optimization is ubiquitous in science and engineering, where function evaluations are costly and the evaluation budget is limited. Traditional evolutionary algorithms and Meta-BlackBox Optimization (MetaBBO) approaches typically consume most evaluations on candidate selection, often wasting precious budget on inferior solutions. Although surrogate-assisted evolution and Bayesian optimization aim to reduce evaluations through surrogate models, constructing an accurate global model from limited data remains challenging, and model bias can easily trap the search in local optima. To overcome these limitations, we propose NeurGO, a generative MetaBBO framework that directly synthesizes elite candidates from historical population states. Specifically, we employ an attention-based encoder to capture the population-level search trend and condition a decoder on this representation to generate high-quality candidates, avoiding the expensive evaluation of large offspring pools. We then design a quality-diversity loss to maintain solution quality and population diversity throughout the search. Through extensive benchmarking on CEC 2008 and the COCO BBOB test suites, our method achieves better optimization performance under the same evaluation budget and exhibits faster convergence.

</details>


### [298] [Separating Capability from Permission: A Governance Framework for Agentic AI Autonomy Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23438)
*Haining Zheng,Qian Dong,Rodolfo K. Depena,Jonathan D. Bhatia,Feng Xiao,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个治理框架，明确区分AI系统"允许的自主级别"(AAL)和"自主能力级别"(ACL)，并通过风险感知决策流程和实际企业案例，展示了如何基于风险、可逆性和组织准备度将高能力系统约束至更低允许自主级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常混淆AI技术上的自主能力与实践中应被允许的自主程度，缺乏明确的治理区分，导致风险、监督和问责考量被忽视。

Method: 提出AAL（允许自主级别）与ACL（自主能力级别）分离的框架；定义从反应执行到授权操作的结构化自主级别体系；设计风险感知的决策流程来分配允许自主级别；通过已部署的企业数据工程代理进行案例验证。

Result: 成功展示了一个高能力级别（ACL）的系统如何在风险、可逆性和组织准备度考量下被有意约束至较低允许自主级别（AAL），验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 区分授权与能力为Agentic AI系统的设计、部署和治理提供了实用指导，有助于在技术能力与安全可控之间取得平衡。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly exhibit agentic behavior, discussions of autonomy often conflate what systems are technically capable of doing with what they should be permitted to do in practice. This paper introduces a governance framework that explicitly separates Allowed Autonomy Levels (AAL), which define the degree of autonomy an AI agent is authorized to exercise given risk, oversight, and accountability considerations, from Autonomous Capability Levels (ACL), which characterize an agent's inherent technical abilities. We present a structured set of autonomy levels spanning reactive execution, decision support, supervised action, goal-directed autonomy, and delegated operational authority, and describe how control, reversibility, and accountability change as autonomy increases. To operationalize this framework, we propose a risk-aware decision process for assigning allowed autonomy, analyze how risk and accountability evolve across autonomy levels, and demonstrate its application through a deployed enterprise data engineering agent, illustrating how a system assessed at a high capability level can be deliberately constrained to a lower allowed autonomy based on risk, reversibility, and organizational readiness. By distinguishing authorization from capability, this work provides practical guidance for the design, deployment, and governance of Agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [299] [Do LLMs Know Their Vulnerable Scenarios?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23496)
*Ziheng Peng,Huiqi Deng,Haoran Jing,Xuankun Rong,Jiahui Han,Xiting Wang,Na Zou,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现场景化提示会激活模型内部的方向向量，从而抑制安全对齐的拒绝机制。基于此提出Concept2Scenario框架，通过稀疏自编码器将概念归因于拒绝抑制，转化为可解释的自然语言场景，并识别场景组合。在多个开源和闭源模型上，发现的场景作为可复用先验显著提升攻击成功率，并跨模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试方法通过观察攻击结果来经验性地识别有效场景，但为何特定场景会削弱拒绝机制在机理上仍不清楚。同时，机理可解释性研究分别刻画了拒绝方向和越狱相关特征，但未解释两者表征之间的关系。

Method: 提出Concept2Scenario框架：（1）用稀疏自编码器实例化宽泛的概念空间；（2）将拒绝抑制归因到单个概念；（3）将识别的概念转化为可解释的自然语言场景；（4）通过交互归因识别协同场景组合。

Result: 在三个开源模型、两个安全基准和六种黑盒越狱方法上，发现的场景作为可复用先验使平均攻击成功率提升高达18.2个百分点。场景可迁移到GPT-5、Claude-Haiku-4.5和Gemini-3-Flash。识别的场景组合优于单个场景，并使迭代攻击在更少轮次内成功。

Conclusion: 场景级拒绝漏洞在跨模型家族间共享，场景组合能更有效地绕过安全对齐，揭示了场景化提示如何通过内部方向向量抑制模型拒绝行为的机制。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet embedding the same requests in particular scenarios can bypass their safeguards. Existing red-teaming methods empirically identify effective scenarios through observed attack outcomes, but why particular scenarios weaken refusal remains mechanistically unclear. Meanwhile, mechanistic interpretability studies have characterized both refusal directions and jailbreak-associated features, without explaining the relationship between the two representations. In this work, we show that scenario-wrapped prompts activate internal scenario directions whose causal steering consistently reduces refusal scores. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{Concept2Scenario}, a concept-based attribution framework for vulnerable scenario discovery. It instantiates a broad concept space with a sparse autoencoder, attributes refusal suppression to individual concepts, translates the identified concepts into interpretable natural-language scenarios, and identifies synergistic scenario combinations through interaction attribution. Across three open-source models, two safety benchmarks, and six black-box jailbreak methods, the discovered scenarios serve as reusable priors that improve average attack success rates by up to $18.2$ percentage points. They also transfer to GPT-5, Claude-Haiku-4.5, and Gemini-3-Flash, suggesting that some scenario-level refusal vulnerabilities are shared across model families. Moreover, the identified combinations outperform their individual constituents and enable iterative attacks to succeed in fewer turns.

</details>


### [300] [Delegation Intelligence in Deep Search: A Controllable Framework for Disentangled Capability Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23524)
*Xinhao Yao,Yuanzhuo Liu,Changhao Wang,Yunfei Yu,Haoran Tan,Yuyao Zhang,Ruifeng Ren,Minlong Peng,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出深度搜索中的"委托智能"概念，将其解耦为搜索决策与信息综合验证两个维度，并构建了可控合成流程 DelegSearchBench 及解耦评估协议，证明仅用最终答案准确率无法充分表征深度搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索评估仅依赖端到端答案准确率，将检索质量、长上下文理解、证据验证和工具使用决策纠缠在一起，难以判断模型是否真正理解何时以及如何将信息寻求委托给搜索。需要一种解耦的评估方法来独立衡量各维度能力。

Method: (1) 形式化定义"委托智能"为搜索决策制定和信息综合验证两个互补维度；(2) 开发基于文档逆向工程的可控合成流程，生成可控深度搜索评估的通用方案；(3) 构建 DelegSearchBench 数据集及解耦评估协议，通过改变文档组合和工具访问来隔离各能力维度。

Result: 在多种代表性模型上证明，仅用最终答案准确率无法充分表征深度搜索能力，解耦评估能揭示模型在不同维度上的真实表现差异。

Conclusion: 深度搜索能力的评估需要超越简单的最终答案准确率，采用解耦的评估方法才能有效衡量模型在搜索决策和信息综合方面的真实能力。

Abstract: Deep search is becoming a core capability of modern agent systems, yet it is typically evaluated solely based on end-to-end answer accuracy. This coupled evaluation paradigm entangles retrieval quality, long-context comprehension, evidence verification, and tool-use decisions, making it difficult to determine whether a model truly knows when and how to delegate information seeking to search. To this end: (1) We formalize this meta-capability as Delegation Intelligence in deep search and decompose it into complementary dimensions-Search Decision-Making (recognizing information insufficiency and deciding whether, when, and how to search) and Information Synthesis and Verification (aggregating evidence from multiple sources, judging source reliability, and synthesizing information under noisy, potentially adversarial conditions). (2) To enable disentangled and reproducible measurement, we develop a controllable synthesis pipeline built on document-grounded reverse engineering. This yields a general recipe for constructing controlled deep-search evaluations rather than a single fixed dataset. (3) As a concrete instantiation, we construct DelegSearchBench, together with a disentangled evaluation protocol that isolates each capability dimension by varying document composition and tool access. (4) Across representative models, we demonstrate that deep-search competence cannot be adequately characterized by final-answer accuracy alone...

</details>


### [301] [ObsDriveBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Understanding under Adverse Weather with Observability Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23537)
*Qiao Yan,Yihan Wang,Zhenghao Xing,Jiaqi Xu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ObsDriveBench——首个面向真实恶劣天气的多模态自动驾驶基准，包含可观测性感知、空间可靠性和风险感知决策三个能力维度，并基于14k训练/13k测试问题评估现有视觉语言模型在雨、雾、雪等条件下的性能退化。同时提出ObsDrive模型，通过正常天气监督微调和恶劣天气强化学习提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准主要在标准条件、合成退化或单模态下评估，缺乏对真实恶劣天气下多模态场景理解与决策行为的研究，特别是环境可观测性退化导致的多模态不一致问题未被充分探索。

Method: 构建ObsDriveBench基准，通过对相机、LiDAR、雷达同步输入进行可观测性元标注和场景描述，设计三类能力导向的选择题任务；提出ObsDrive模型，采用正常天气监督微调+恶劣天气强化学习的两阶段训练策略。

Result: 现有视觉语言模型在三种能力维度上均出现一致性的性能退化；ObsDrive模型在所有三个能力上均提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 真实恶劣天气下多模态可观测性退化是影响VLM自动驾驶性能的关键因素，面向可观测性的基准设计与针对性训练策略能有效提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving under adverse weather remains a critical challenge, yet existing vision-language benchmarks mainly evaluate under standard conditions, synthetic corruptions, or single modality. As a result, it remains unclear how vision-language models behave under real-world adverse weather with multi-modal inputs. We argue that a key difficulty lies in degraded environmental observability: under fog, rain, snow, and low illumination, multi-modal observations become unreliable and cross-modally inconsistent, posing challenges to scene understanding, and subsequent decision-making. To study this, we introduce \textbf{ObsDriveBench}, a real-world multi-modal benchmark for adverse-weather autonomous driving. Our benchmark is designed with three capability dimensions: \textbf{observability awareness}, \textbf{spatial reliability}, and \textbf{risk-aware decision-making}, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of model behavior under degraded observations. We construct the benchmark through observability meta-annotation, scene description, and capability oriented multiple-choice tasks over synchronized camera, LiDAR, and radar inputs, forming a benchmark with over 14k training and 13k test questions. Experiments reveal consistent performance degradation of existing vision-language models. We further introduce \textbf{ObsDrive} model with normal-weather supervised fine-tuning and adverse-weather reinforcement learning, improving robustness across all three capabilities. The dataset and evaluation code will be released at \href{https://github.com/russellyq/ObsDriveBench}{\texttt{ObsDriveBench}}.

</details>


### [302] [Verification-Notebook Learning for Source-Aware Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23581)
*Junyuan Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出验证笔记本学习（VNL），一种非参数框架，通过为冻结的大型视觉语言模型构建外部验证笔记本，积累多模态虚假信息验证的经验知识，无需模型训练即可提升验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态虚假信息验证具有挑战性，不同来源的误导信号需要不同形式的证据。现有方法通过提示、检索或推理改进验证过程，但很少保留从先前示例中学到的验证模式。

Method: VNL是一个非参数框架，在推理前为冻结的LVLM构建外部验证笔记本。笔记本紧凑地记录决策原则、证据线索和反复出现的陷阱，推理期间保持固定并指导新示例的验证。知识存储在可直接检查的工件中，而非更新模型参数或存储演示。

Result: 实验表明VNL持续优于一系列竞争基线。进一步分析显示，验证笔记本在保持紧凑和可解释的同时，改善了细粒度的来源归因。

Conclusion: VNL提供了一种无需模型训练即可积累验证知识的有效方法，通过可解释的笔记本形式记录验证经验，提升多模态虚假信息验证性能。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation verification is challenging because misleading signals may come from different parts of a post and require different forms of evidence. LVLMs are well suited to this task, but their verification performance often depends on the inference procedure applied to each instance. Existing methods improve this procedure through stronger prompting, retrieval, or deliberation, but rarely retain the verification patterns learned from previous examples. We propose Verification-Notebook Learning (VNL), a non-parametric framework that learns an external verification procedure for a frozen LVLM before inference. VNL builds a compact notebook of decision principles, evidence cues, and recurring pitfalls from prior verification experience. The notebook remains fixed during inference and guides the verification of new examples. Rather than updating model parameters or storing demonstrations, VNL records learned knowledge in an artifact that can be inspected directly. Experiments show that VNL consistently outperforms a range of competitive baselines. Further analyses show that the Verification Notebook improves fine-grained source attribution while remaining compact and interpretable, providing an effective way to accumulate verification knowledge without model training.

</details>


### [303] [Are You Still the Agent I Authorized? Earned Authority under a Fixed Ceiling for Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23586)
*Zhaoxi Zhang,Xiaomei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出授权连续性（authorization continuity）概念，针对长寿命AI代理在部署后演化（如获取技能、修改工作流）导致的授权问题，建立了状态边界模型（state-bound model），定义了在代理突变时授权何时保持有效、权限如何变化以及不可逾越的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命AI代理在部署后持续演化（保留经验、获取技能和工具、修改工作流、委托任务等），这虽然提升了适应性，但带来了独特的授权问题：当代理在已有授权下演化时，行使权限的主体或上下文可能与用户最初评估的不再匹配，演化可能改变旧授权可触及的效果范围以及任务所需的权限级别，而现有工具策略虽然约束行为，却无法判断授权是否应在变化后继续有效。

Method: 提出状态边界模型（state-bound model），在授权时设定一个转移包络（transition envelope）和不可变的效果上限（effect ceiling）。包络决定授权在代理突变后是否仍然有效；效果上限之下，权限可以自由收缩，但扩展需满足特定证据条件。区分请求效果与实现效果，并在完全中介、合理的效应抽象、衰减式委托和监控完整性的条件下，证明突变无法将受保护效果放大到用户设定的上限之上。代理产生的证据可以在上限之下分配权限，但不能提高上限。此外，映射了六种突变类型及其授权后果。

Result: 证明在完全中介、合理的效应抽象、衰减式委托和监控完整性四个条件下，代理突变不会将受保护效果放大到用户设定的效果上限之上；代理产生的证据只能在上限之下分配权限，不能提高上限；给出了六种突变类别的授权后果映射。

Conclusion: 提出授权连续性问题并给出形式化模型，为长寿命AI代理的演化过程中的授权管理提供了理论基础和约束条件，确保代理演化不会突破用户最初设定的安全边界。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents increasingly evolve after deployment by retaining experience, acquiring skills and tools, revising workflows, delegating work, and moving across task phases. This improves adaptation but creates a distinct authorization problem. Tool-enabled agents can turn model errors and prompt injections into consequential external actions; when evolution occurs under a live grant, the subject exercising that authority or the context in which it acts may no longer match what the user evaluated. Evolution can change both the effects reachable under an old grant and the authority required by the task, which may rise, fall, or become incomparable. Existing tool policies constrain actions but do not determine when a grant survives this change.
  We formulate authorization continuity: when does an existing grant remain valid, how may active authority change, and what boundary must never move? Our state-bound model fixes a transition envelope and an immutable effect ceiling at grant time. The envelope determines whether the grant survives a mutation; below the ceiling, authority may contract freely and expand only under specified evidence conditions. We distinguish requested from realized effects and prove that, under complete mediation, sound effect abstraction, attenuating delegation, and monitor integrity, mutation cannot amplify protected effects beyond the user-issued ceiling. Agent-produced evidence may allocate authority below the ceiling but cannot raise it. Finally, we map six mutation classes to their authorization consequences.

</details>


### [304] [Hybrid Advantage Estimation with Unified Critic for VLM Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23605)
*Wenxuan Zhang,Yuhui Wang,Donggang Jia,Xiaoqian Shen,Jian Ding,Ivan Viola,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyGAE，一种基于演员-评论家框架的方法，通过混合优势函数和统一评论家模型联合优化令牌级和回合级目标，在多轮决策环境中平均成功率91%，相比其他方法提升10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖拼接令牌轨迹的令牌级优化或回合内均匀信用的回合级优化，缺乏同时优化两个层面目标的统一框架。

Method: 建立令牌级和回合级优化的理论公式，推导混合优势函数；通过合理选择折扣因子和学习目标，证明统一评论家模型可同时估计两种层级的值函数；提出HyGAE演员-评论家框架联合优化两个目标。

Result: 在五个多轮决策环境中平均成功率91%，比现有方法提升10%，验证了混合优势和回报精确解析形式的重要性。

Conclusion: HyGAE通过联合优化令牌和回合级目标有效提升了VLM在多轮交互环境中的决策能力。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) now act as agents in interactive environments, where success requires coherent reasoning and decision-making across turns. Although end-to-end training in agentic environments can improve such multi-turn decision-making abilities, current methods mainly rely on either token-wise optimization over concatenated token trajectories or turn-wise optimization with uniform within-turn credit. In this work, we establish theoretical formulations for the two levels of optimization and derive a hybrid advantage that serves both objectives. Furthermore, with an appropriate choice of discount factor and learning target, we prove that a unified critic model can estimate values for both turn-wise and token-wise. As such, we propose HyGAE, an actor-critic framework that jointly optimizes token- and turn-level objectives with the hybrid advantage and unified critic. We conduct extensive evaluations of HyGAE across five multi-turn decision-making environments, where it achieves an average success rate of 91% and a significant improvement of 10% over other methods. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth analysis showing that the exact analytic form of the hybrid advantage and return is crucial for optimization. Project Page: https://wx-zhang.github.io/hygae-web/.

</details>


### [305] [SpecAHD: Localize to Specialize for Automated Heuristic Design in Large-Scale Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23676)
*Kezhao Lai,Yutao Lai,Hai-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecAHD，一种耦合双层框架用于路由问题中的实例内启发式专业化。上层搜索学习暴露有界修复区域，下层进化互补的可执行启发式程序库。通过单调子模目标实现贪心选择，在四个路由问题中将目标成本降低高达57.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模路由问题中，局部重构虽然缩小了每个优化任务的规模，但同一当前解中的修复区域结构差异显著，单一构造规则无法兼顾所有区域，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出耦合双层框架SpecAHD：上层搜索确定有界修复区域的暴露位置（即生成修复任务），下层进化针对这些任务的可执行启发式程序库。下层目标函数在固定上层程序和候选池下具有单调子模性，支持贪心选择并保证(1-1/e)近似比。

Result: 在四个路由问题和多个LLM骨干上，SpecAHD相比最强竞争基线将目标成本降低高达57.7%，并在多数公开实例上超越每实例基线包络。

Conclusion: 实例内专业化框架有效解决大规模路由问题中单一规则难以适应异构修复区域的问题，提供理论保证且显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: LLM-based automated heuristic design (AHD) typically scores executable programs on complete instances or within fixed solver components. In large-scale routing problems, localized reconstruction reduces the size of each optimization task, but repair regions within the same incumbent can exhibit substantially different structures. One construction rule must therefore compromise across them. In this paper, we propose SpecAHD, a coupled bilevel framework for within-instance specialization. An upper-level search learns where to expose bounded repair regions, while a lower-level search evolves a complementary repertoire of executable heuristics for the induced repair tasks. The upper-level program determines the repair tasks seen by the lower level, while checked repair outcomes determine how upper-level programs are evaluated. The lower-level objective favors heuristics that perform well on average or solve tasks that the current repertoire handles poorly. For the repair tasks induced by a fixed upper-level program and a fixed lower-level candidate pool, this objective is monotone submodular, allowing greedy repertoire selection with a (1-1/e) approximation guarantee. Across four routing problems and multiple LLM backbones, SpecAHD reduces held-out objective cost by up to 57.7% against the strongest competing AHD baseline and outperforms the per-instance baseline envelope on most public instances.

</details>


### [306] [Focus Is All You Need: Adaptive Goal-aware Attention Orchestration for Multi-Agent Graph Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23678)
*Mingzhou Fan,Siyuan Xu,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出注意力编排（Attention Orchestration）范式及自适应目标感知注意力编排（AGAO）框架，通过目标感知、拓扑感知和资源感知三种注意力机制，将静态智能体图转化为动态自适应系统，在多样化的多智能体工作负载上提升任务效果并减少计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图编排的多智能体系统在工作流增长时存在注意力分配问题——均匀执行所有图组件会浪费资源在无关或低影响任务上，亟需一种动态聚焦关键推理路径的机制。

Method: AGAO框架包含三种注意力组件：(1) 目标感知注意力——衡量用户目标与智能体能力的语义相关性；(2) 拓扑感知注意力——建模智能体图的结构依赖关系；(3) 资源感知注意力——跨异构智能体分配预算和执行优先级。三者协同将静态图转化为自适应系统。

Result: 在多样化多智能体工作负载上，AGAO相比现有基于图的执行策略，提升了任务有效性，同时减少了不必要的计算、延迟和token消耗。

Conclusion: 本文建立了"注意力工程"（Attention Engineering）作为可扩展、智能多智能体系统的一个研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable autonomous agents for reasoning, planning, and tool use. Recent systems increasingly organize these agents as graphs of specialized, interconnected nodes. Although graph-based orchestration supports flexible decomposition and coordination, it creates a key challenge: \textbf{attention allocation}. As workflows grow, existing approaches often execute graph components uniformly, wasting resources on irrelevant or low-impact tasks. We introduce \textbf{Attention Orchestration}, a paradigm that extends Transformer-style attention from token representations to workflow-level agent coordination. Our framework, \textbf{Adaptive Goal-aware Attention Orchestration (AGAO)}, dynamically estimates agent importance based on user objectives, graph dependencies, and computational constraints. AGAO combines three components: (1) goal-aware attention, measuring semantic relevance between user goals and agent capabilities; (2) topology-aware attention, modeling structural dependencies in agent graphs; and (3) resource-aware attention, allocating budgets and execution priorities across heterogeneous agents. Together, these mechanisms transform static agent graphs into adaptive systems that focus computation on goal-critical reasoning paths. Experiments across diverse multi-agent workloads show that AGAO improves task effectiveness while reducing unnecessary computation, latency, and token consumption compared with existing graph-based execution strategies. Our work establishes \textbf{Attention Engineering} as a direction for scalable, intelligent multi-agent systems. Code: https://github.com/MingzhouFan97/AGAO.

</details>


### [307] [Compute Globally, Materialize Locally: The Memory Contract of Sparse Event-KV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23693)
*Zefeng Cai,Zerui Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义物化"概念，证明KV缓存中的下游事件行可在源观测被删除后独立承载语义信息。通过精心构造的无答案事件可将恢复率从6%提升至51%，而自然提及无效。这揭示了稀疏事件KV服务的内存契约：该写什么、写在哪、源消失后什么能存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时域智能体重用KV缓存作为记忆时，依赖一个未经严格检验的前提：源观测被删除后，保留的事件依然具有信息价值。作者希望直接检验这一假设，并探究KV缓存中保留行的语义承载能力。

Method: 在完全相同的智能体历史中，省略一个早期观测条目，测试模型在感知到该遗漏时的回答行为。通过对比自然提及与精心构造的无答案事件，量化语义物化效应的存在与强度。

Result: 被遗漏的值主导了模型回答（尽管服务中没有指示正确值）；构造性无答案事件将捐赠者对位恢复率从6%提升至51%；自然提及无显著优势；紧凑状态可存活，较大负载衰减至随机水平；写入与否取决于措辞而非语义理解。

Conclusion: 证实了语义物化现象的存在，给出了稀疏事件KV服务的内存契约：需关注如何写入、写入位置以及源消失后的信息存活特性。对于丢弃缓存条目的系统而言，观测到无精度损失并不等价于源事件是无关的。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly reuse their KV cache as memory: a serving system keeps a subset of cached entries and drops the rest. Eviction and episodic-memory schemes therefore rest on a premise rarely tested directly, that a retained event is still informative once the observations that produced it are gone. We test it by omitting one earlier observation from what is served, across otherwise identical agent histories. Among items sensitive to that observation, the answer overwhelmingly follows the omitted value, though no served span says which value is correct. We call this semantic materialization: a downstream event's cached rows act as an independently servable view of computation whose inputs are gone. It can also be written on purpose. A deliberately phrased, answer-free event raises donor-aligned recovery from 6% to 51% on Qwen3-8B without ever naming the value, whereas passively harvesting natural mentions from long-term dialog yields no detected advantage. What such a row carries is specific and bounded. Compact state survives, larger payloads decay toward chance, and whether a construction writes at all turns on phrasing rather than on meaning alone, so two phrasings the model comprehends equally well can diverge sharply. The result is a memory contract for sparse event-KV serving: what to write, where it lands, and what survives once the source is gone. For anyone who evicts the corollary is that dropping a source event and observing no accuracy loss does not show the source was unnecessary.

</details>


### [308] [Offline-to-Online Creative Optimization with Generative Models and Adaptive Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23696)
*Kevin Lee,Benjamin Letham,Zhiyuan Jerry Lin,Elodie Samson,Eric Onofrey,Poppy Zhang,Shawndra Hill,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线到在线的广告创意优化工作流：利用历史A/B测试数据训练预测模型，作为生成模型的推理时评判器来指导创意生成与筛选，再通过在线自适应实验进行评估。在50臂现场实验中，该方法生成的最佳创意相比人类作者的最佳创意提升了45.1%的参与度，并在另外两个实验中分别获得46.7%和36.2%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告创意优化日益受限于评估环节而非生成环节——生成模型虽能产出大量候选创意，但可靠评估依赖在线实验，而在线实验只能测试有限数量的候选。因此需要研究如何利用历史A/B测试数据来生成和筛选候选创意。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：基于历史实验数据训练预测模型，作为生成模型的推理时评判器，对生成模型产出的创意进行排序和优化；2) 在线阶段：将最终筛选出的测试组部署到在线自适应实验中。

Result: 1) 50臂现场实验中，该方法生成的最佳创意比人类最佳创意参与度提升45.1%；2) 两个额外实验分别提升46.7%和36.2%；3) 预测模型虽有噪声无法直接离线识别最佳创意，但能有效指导生成模型产出强候选。

Conclusion: 提出创意优化的设计原则：使用预测模型指导生成测试组；通过测试组是否包含高性能候选来评判；使用自适应实验在候选间选择，同时限制流量浪费在弱臂上。

Abstract: Ad creative optimization is increasingly constrained by evaluation rather than generation. Generative models can produce many plausible creatives, but reliable evaluation requires online experiments, in which only a limited slate can be tested. We study how to use data from historical A/B tests to generate and select the candidates in that slate. We developed and deployed a performance-driven offline-to-online workflow that guides creative generation with a predictive model as an inference-time critic. In the offline phase, we use a predictive model trained on historical experiments to rank and refine variants created by a generative model. A final test slate is then deployed in an online adaptive experiment. In a 50-arm field experiment, we found that the best creative generated with this method yielded 45.1% higher engagement than the best human-authored creative. Two additional experiments showed the same upper-tail pattern, with lifts of 46.7% and 36.2%. We found that despite the predictive model being too noisy to directly identify the best creative offline, it effectively guides the generative model toward creating strong candidates that can be efficiently evaluated in an adaptive experiment. The results suggest a design principle for creative optimization with generative models: use predictive models to guide generation of a slate to test, judge the slate by whether it contains high-performing candidates at a feasible test size, and use adaptive experiments to select among candidates while limiting traffic lost to weak arms.

</details>


### [309] [Offline-Online Curriculum RL for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23700)
*Wendi Deng,Hang Du,Guoshun Nan,Haokun Tian,Jiaqi Yu,Xinlei Cao,Jaile Li,Jingfeng Chen,Ling Deng,Ting Li,Hao Yang,Jun Liu,Xudong Jiang,Sicong Leng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出O²-CritiCuRL框架，通过离线多轮滚动分析识别关键推理步骤，再结合在线渐进式强化学习引导模型关注关键步骤，在多模态推理任务上达到SOTA并提升训练与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在推理任务中虽能给出正确答案，但中间步骤存在缺陷，表明模型依赖虚假捷径而非忠实推理，缺乏可解释性和可靠性；现有步级监督方法难以区分关键步骤与冗余步骤。

Method: 提出O²-CritiCuRL框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段：对带步骤标注的轨迹进行多次滚动分析，估计步级重要性，蒸馏关键推理步骤并过滤冗余步骤；(2) 在线阶段：采用渐进式步级强化学习策略，通过截断链引导模型推断缺失步骤并修正推理，强化对关键步骤的关注。

Result: 在多模态推理基准上达到SOTA性能，同时实现更优的训练和推理效率。

Conclusion: 引入关键步骤意识的课程强化学习框架能有效提升模型推理的忠实性和效率，克服静态监督的局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models exhibit capabilities on reasoning tasks, yet often produce flawed intermediate steps while yielding correct final answers. This behavior undermines interpretability and reliability, suggesting reliance on spurious shortcuts rather than faithful reasoning. Although efforts have explored step-level supervision, distinguishing decisive steps from redundant ones remains challenging. We propose $O^2$-CritiCuRL, a novel curriculum reinforcement learning framework that introduces critical-step awareness through an iterative offline-online paradigm. In the offline stage, $O^2$-CritiCuRL conducts multi-rollout analysis over step-annotated trajectories to estimate step-level importance, allowing the framework to distill critical reasoning steps and filter out redundant ones. In the online stage, we employ a progressive step-level reinforcement learning strategy, where truncated chains guide the model to infer missing steps and refine its reasoning, thereby sharpening its focus on critical steps and overcoming the limitations of static supervision. Extensive experiments on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance while delivering superior training and inference efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/kk0013/CritiCuRL.

</details>


### [310] [E-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Step Tool-Use Agents in Real-World Product Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23722)
*Weihuang Zheng,Tianyuan Zou,Eileen Ye,Alphet Liu,Youyong Kong,Ya-Qin Zhang,Duran Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: E-Bench是一个完全合成的基准测试，包含323个跨三个产品领域的多步状态变更任务，用于评估LLM在多步工具使用中的能力。实验表明，最强模型的Pass^3低于60%，即使引入代码执行扩展也低于70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在评估LLM作为工具使用智能体时，往往局限于孤立的API调用、短轨迹或难以扩展/控制的设置，缺乏对多步状态变更环境下工具使用能力的系统评估。

Method: 采用图引导的数据库填充构建可复用、无孤岛的产品环境，并通过生成器-求解器非对称性构造同时存在信息差距和工具差距的任务，最终通过数据库状态差异进行确定性评分。

Result: 对11个前沿LLM的评估显示，最强模型在多步工具使用上的Pass^3低于60%，E-Bench-Code扩展下也低于70%。

Conclusion: 多步工具使用对当前LLM仍具挑战性，E-Bench作为可控、可扩展的合成基准，为该能力的评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents that interact with stateful environments over multiple steps: gathering hidden information, composing tool calls, and committing state changes. We refer to this capability as multi-step tool use. Existing benchmarks have advanced tool-use agent evaluation, but often focus on isolated API calls, short trajectories, or settings that are difficult to scale or control. We introduce E-Bench, a fully synthetic benchmark with 323 state-changing tasks across three product domains: Honor of Kings, QQ Music, and Tencent Meeting. E-Bench decouples environment synthesis from task synthesis: graph-guided database filling builds reusable, orphan-free product environments, while generator-solver asymmetry creates tasks with both an information gap and a tool gap, requiring agents to discover hidden data and compose multiple tool calls before changing state. Outcomes are graded deterministically by database-state diffs. Since both environments and tasks are synthetic, E-Bench is controllable at the environment level and scalable at the task level. Benchmarking 11 cutting-edge LLMs shows that multi-step tool use remains challenging: Pass^3 stays below 60% for the strongest models, and even with code execution in the E-Bench-Code extension, reliability (Pass^3) remains below 70%.

</details>


### [311] [Training Language Models to Cooperate with Inference-Time Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23771)
*Moumita Choudhury,Vanshaj Khattar,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Ankush Chakrabarty,Shlomo Zilberstein,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CALM是一个面向LLM的控制器感知后训练框架，通过将多种推理控制器（如CoT、自一致性、辩论、规划、验证等）纳入训练循环，采用多任务强化学习与模块化分解策略，解决传统单控制器优化与部署时多控制器使用之间的不匹配问题，提升跨推理工作流的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法通常针对单一固定交互模式（如仅CoT）进行优化，但实际部署中依赖多样化的推理控制器（如CoT、自一致性、辩论、规划、验证等），导致训练与部署之间存在不匹配，限制了模型向新推理工作流的迁移能力。

Method: 提出CALM框架，将控制器感知后训练形式化为多任务强化学习问题，控制器被建模为可复用局部推理模块的组合；利用这种模块化结构，在回合级GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）目标下实现混合控制器训练，支持控制器感知与模块感知的训练策略。

Result: 在保留的控制器组合和更广泛的控制器迁移场景上评估，结果表明控制器感知后训练相比单控制器优化，能显著提升模型跨不同推理工作流的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将控制器显式纳入训练循环是提升LLM对多样化推理工作流泛化能力的有效策略，控制器感知后训练优于传统的单控制器优化方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) performance increasingly depends not only on the base model, but also on the inference-time controller used to organize reasoning. Existing post-training methods, however, typically optimize for a single fixed interaction pattern, despite real deployments relying on diverse controllers such as Chain-of-Thought, self-consistency, debate, planning, and verification pipelines. This creates a training--deployment mismatch and limits transfer to new workflows. We introduce CALM (Controller-Aware Language Models), a post-training framework that explicitly places controllers in the training loop. We formulate controller-aware post-training as multi-task reinforcement learning over controller-induced interaction protocols, where controllers are compositions of reusable local reasoning modules. This structure also induces a module-level decomposition of mixed-controller training under a turn-level GRPO objective, enabling a systematic study of controller and module-aware training strategies. We evaluate CALM on held-out controller compositions and broader controller shifts, showing that controller-aware post-training improves generalization across inference-time workflows beyond single-controller optimization.

</details>


### [312] [From RLVR to RLSVR: Task Transformation Induces Self-Verifiable Rewards for Open-Ended LLM Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23802)
*Qinsi Wang,Jing Shi,Huazheng Wang,Kun Wan,Yiran Wu,Bo Liu,Qingyun Wu,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen,Handong Zhao,Wentian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLSVR（基于自验证奖励的强化学习）框架，通过将开放任务转化为可验证的代理环境（如多智能体自博弈游戏SpyRL），将RLVR扩展到非数学/编程领域。实验表明在文本摘要、创意写作和数学推理上优于现有自改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR仅适用于数学和编程等可确定性验证的领域，而开放任务依赖人工偏好、奖励模型或LLM裁判，存在评估偏差、裁判能力瓶颈和额外推理成本。受自监督学习启发，作者希望将RLVR扩展到开放任务。

Method: 提出RLSVR框架，通过任务转换将开放任务变为可验证的代理环境。具体实例化为SpyRL——一个受"谁是卧底"启发的多智能体自博弈环境：智能体拥有不对称信息，完成相同目标任务后投票识别指定卧底，投票结果提供完全可验证的奖励信号。

Result: 在文本摘要、创意写作和数学推理任务上，SpyRL优于现有针对非可验证任务的自改进方法，并在可验证推理任务上持续提升。

Conclusion: 任务转换范式能够将可扩展的RLVR自改进扩展到超越固有可验证领域之外，为开放任务的强化学习优化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has driven recent progress in reasoning-oriented large language models (LLMs) by enabling large-scale optimization. However, its applicability remains largely limited to domains such as mathematics and coding, where correctness can be deterministically verified. Open-ended tasks instead often rely on human preferences, reward models, or LLM-based judges, introducing evaluation bias, judge capability bottlenecks, and additional inference costs.Drawing on the principle of self-supervised learning, which constructs pretext tasks to derive supervision from the data itself, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Self-Verifiable Rewards (RLSVR), a task-transformation-based training paradigm for extending RLVR to open-ended tasks. RLSVR transforms open-ended tasks into verifiable proxy environments whose internal rules and interaction outcomes automatically generate reward signals. We instantiate RLSVR with SpyRL, a multi-agent self-play environment inspired by Who Is the Spy?. Agents receive asymmetric information, complete the same target task, and vote to identify a designated spy. Because the spy identity is predetermined, voting outcomes provide fully verifiable rewards, while successful identification remains closely related to output quality. Experiments on text summarization, creative writing, and mathematical reasoning show that SpyRL outperforms existing self-improvement methods on non-verifiable tasks and yields consistent gains on verifiable reasoning tasks. These results demonstrate that task transformation can extend scalable RLVR-based self-improvement beyond inherently verifiable domains. Models and code have been released at https://github.com/wangqinsi1/SpyRL.

</details>


### [313] [ACM: Agentic Context Management for Long Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23809)
*Xiaochuan Li,Ryan Ming,Meng Chu,Shuai Shao,Rong Jin,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Context Management (ACM)框架，让智能体自主管理上下文，通过外部记忆系统实现无损压缩，在搜索和编码任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法存在信息损失且依赖刚性启发式规则，无法与智能体不断演化的推理焦点对齐。

Method: 受人类短期/长期记忆交互启发，ACM让智能体自主决策何时压缩上下文，将丢弃内容卸载到外部记忆系统并可按需查询；同时构建后训练流程来生成高质量的上下文管理示范。

Result: 在智能体搜索和编码任务上改进模型性能；减少峰值token压力，支持更长的探索，跨独立试验产生更一致的解决方案。

Conclusion: ACM框架通过自主、无损的上下文管理有效提升了智能体在长程、多轮任务上的表现。

Abstract: Agentic tasks are inherently long-horizon and multi-turn, constantly accumulating context through interactions with the environment. Existing context compression methods inevitably incur information loss and are triggered by rigid heuristic rules, leaving them misaligned with the agent's evolving reasoning focus. We propose Agentic Context Management (ACM), a framework that equips agents with purpose-built context editing tools for lossless context management. Inspired by the interaction between short-term and long-term human memory, the agent autonomously decides when to compress its context, offloads discarded content to an external memory system, and queries it on demand for later retrieval. Building on this framework, we further develop a post-training pipeline that constructs high-quality demonstrations of context management and improves model performance on both agentic search and coding tasks. Further analysis reveals that effective context management reduces peak token pressure, enables extended explorations, and yields more consistent solutions across independent trials. Code, data, and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/lixiaochuan2020/agentic-context-management.

</details>


### [314] [Do Visual Features Improve Other-Initiated Repair Detection? A Dyadic Multimodal Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23845)
*Anh Ngo,Nicolas Rollet,Catherine Pelachaud,Chloé Clavel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态模型，通过整合对话分析中的视觉特征（如目光、面部表情、身体姿态和手势），用于检测和分类他人引发的自我修复（OIR）。在两个不同语言和交互场景的语料库上验证，视觉信息持续提升了基于文本和音频基线的性能，并揭示了跨模态特征贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 他人引发的自我修复（OIR）是对话交互中解决沟通故障的关键机制，但现有计算方法主要依赖文本和音频，忽略了对话分析研究中已证实的非语言信号（如目光、表情、手势等）对OIR的重要作用。

Method: 构建了一个多模态模型，融合来自对话分析的视觉特征（目光转移、面部表情、身体姿态、手势），结合文本和音频模态，对OIR进行检测与分类。

Result: 在两个涵盖不同语言和交互场景的语料库上，视觉信息持续提升了模型性能，超越了仅使用文本和音频的基线方法。

Conclusion: 视觉模态对OIR检测与分类具有稳定的正向贡献，跨模态特征在不同语料库中展现出不同的贡献模式，为对话系统中更高效的沟通故障修复提供了新思路。

Abstract: Other-initiated Self-repair, or in short Other-initiated Repair (OIR), is an essential mechanism in conversational interaction, whereby a recipient signals a problem in speaking, hearing, or understanding, prompting the previous speaker to resolve it. In the case of conversational agents, it is essential to accurately identify these repair initiation strategies to address communication breakdowns efficiently. While conversational analysis studies have shown that OIR initiation is accompanied by both verbal and non-verbal signals such as gaze shifts, facial expressions, body postures, and hand gestures, existing computational approaches rely mainly on text and audio. This paper introduces a novel multimodal model for OIR detection and classification, incorporating a set of visual features drawn from conversation analysis. We evaluate our approach on two corpora with distinct languages and interaction settings. Results demonstrate that visual information consistently improves performance over text and audio baselines, and provide insights into cross-modal feature contributions across two corpora.

</details>


### [315] [Understanding Human-like Solutions in Combinatorial Optimization via Learning and Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23854)
*Haijiang Yan,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Liqiang Huang,Ming Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模行为与计算研究，分析了人类在欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP）中生成近优解的行为特征，发现人类解并非最优但处于近优几何盆地，并提出了结合监督学习、强化学习和测试时搜索的神经策略（基于Pointer Networks）来模拟人类解的最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类如何在计算资源有限的条件下快速找到组合优化问题（如TSP）的接近最优解，以及如何通过计算模型学习这种类人解。

Method: 大规模采样TSP实例，收集人类解，并训练基于Pointer Networks的神经策略，比较多种训练目标：强化学习（RL）、最优解监督学习、人类解监督学习、以及最优预训练后RL微调。使用Best-of-N采样解码。

Result: 人类解与最优解共享许多结构特性，但存在系统性偏差，处于近优几何盆地。最佳模拟人类解的模型是最优预训练后经RL微调、并通过Best-of-N采样的模型。

Conclusion: 类人解可能源于结构化监督学习、强化学习与测试时搜索的组合，这与现代AI系统的计算原理一致。

Abstract: Humans often find good solutions to combinatorial optimization problems that are computationally hard even for advanced computer algorithms. In the Euclidean traveling salesman problems (TSP), people rapidly produce tours that are near-optimal, despite severe limits on time and computation. What makes a tour human-like, and how might such solutions be learned? Here we address these questions through a large-scale behavioral and computational investigation of human performance in Euclidean TSP. We sampled a broad space of TSP instances, collected human solutions, and compared them with neural policies based on Pointer Networks, which are recurrent neural networks with an attention-based pointing mechanism that define probability distributions over valid tours. We trained these networks under multiple objectives, including reinforcement learning (RL), supervised learning from optimal tours, supervised learning from human tours, and RL fine-tuning after optimal-supervised pretraining. Human tours were not identical to optimal tours, but occupied a near-optimal geometric basin: they shared many structural properties with optimal solutions while preserving systematic human-specific deviations. The best account of human tours was not direct imitation of optimal tours, but a model pretrained on optimal tours, fine-tuned by RL, and decoded through $\text{Best-of-}N$ sampling. These findings suggest that human-like solutions may emerge from a combination of structured supervised learning, RL, and test-time search, echoing computational principles underlying many modern artificial intelligence systems.

</details>


### [316] [Cost-Aware Recovery-Pathway Identification and Bayesian Optimization for Autonomous Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23896)
*Debajyoti Ray,Niranjan Srinivas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Coactive Learning方法，将自主实验室的实验决策建模为两阶段序列决策问题（离散路径识别+连续路径内优化），结合成本敏感的贝叶斯假设判别（EC2）和高斯过程贝叶斯优化，在固定预算下实现路径识别与优化的协同。在PNNL的CICERO选择性沉淀研究等基准上，该方法性能接近oracle路径贝叶斯优化，优于先提交后优化的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室虽能自动化实验执行，但关键挑战在于如何决定优先优化哪条回收路径（pathway）。传统方法要么先验选择一条路径（可能选错），要么每条路径都尝试（成本高）。需要一种能在路径识别和路径内优化之间高效分配有限预算的决策框架。

Method: 将问题形式化为两阶段序列决策：离散路径识别阶段（确定哪个反应路径最有前景）和连续路径内优化阶段（在该路径内优化实验条件）。采用Coactive Learning方法，结合EC2（成本敏感贝叶斯假设判别策略）进行路径识别，以及高斯过程贝叶斯优化进行路径内优化。在固定预算下，预算被划分为路径识别成本（上限由EC2边界保证）和路径内优化预算。

Result: 在合成基准（基于PNNL CICERO选择性沉淀研究）上，Coactive Learning性能与使用oracle路径的贝叶斯优化相当，也优于使用首板（first plate）进行路径判别的强基线方法。在NdFeB启发案例中，该方法避免了先提交（commit-first）基线因错误选择氢氧化物路径而导致的性能惩罚。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段协同决策框架能有效避免先验路径选择错误的风险，在有限预算下实现接近oracle水平的优化性能。结论对成本模型的假设敏感，需进一步表征其稳健性。代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories automate experimental execution, but a campaign must also decide which recovery pathway merits optimization. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem with a discrete pathway-identification stage and a continuous within-pathway optimization stage under heterogeneous experimental costs. Our implementation, Coactive learning, combines a cost-sensitive Bayesian hypothesis-discrimination policy motivated by EC2 (Golovin et al., 2010) with Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (Srinivas et al., 2010). Under explicitly stated assumptions, the expected spend of one fixed-budget campaign attempt is bounded by the expected pathway-identification cost plus the capped within-pathway optimization budget. We evaluate the method on synthetic benchmarks constrained by selected results reported for PNNL's CICERO selective-precipitation study (Ritchhart et al., 2026). The method performs comparably to an oracle-pathway Bayesian-optimization reference and to a strong split-plate baseline that discriminates pathways with its first plate, without receiving an oracle label for the correct pathway. It is given a candidate hypothesis space and a diagnostic likelihood model. On an NdFeB-inspired instance, it avoids the simulated penalty of a commit-first baseline that initially selects a plausible but inferior hydroxide pathway. This hypothetical wrong-first-commitment scenario is motivated by the hydroxide-oxalate performance contrast reported by CICERO. We characterize the sensitivity of these conclusions to the assumed cost model. The code and benchmark are open source.

</details>


### [317] [GOTS: Greedy Orthogonal Token Selection for High-Resolution Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23913)
*Jun Ling,Tao Huang,Junzhuo Liu,Bowen Tang,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GOTS方法，通过正交化贪心选择视觉token中残差能量最大的token，在无需训练、不依赖查询的条件下实现高分辨率VLM的高效token压缩，在多个模型和基准上取得优于现有方法的性能保持率，并降低了首token生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型依赖高分辨率视觉编码产生大量视觉token，显著增加了语言模型推理成本。现有token缩减方法主要通过token级重要性、查询相关性、覆盖度、成对多样性或子集级目标来评估token效用，但缺乏从所选子集几何互补性角度系统性地进行token选择。

Method: 提出Greedy Orthogonal Token Selection (GOTS)，一种无需训练、不依赖查询的方法。核心思路是将视觉token缩减视为已选子集张成的子空间互补性问题：每步选择与当前已选token张成子空间正交的残差能量最大的token。该选择规则等价于最大化候选添加的一步Gram行列式增量，为每一步贪心选择提供了精确的局部几何保证。

Result: 在Qwen-VL和InternVL系列的5个高分辨率VLM主干网络以及11个多样化基准上，GOTS实现了比最强基线方法更高的平均性能保持率。在OCRBench上的受控研究表明，GOTS在考虑了选择开销后，降低了模型端的首token生成时间（TTFT）。

Conclusion: GOTS通过正交互补性视角实现高效的视觉token缩减，在无需训练和查询的条件下取得更优的性能保持和更低的推理延迟。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly rely on dynamic or high-resolution visual encoding, producing thousands of visual tokens that substantially increase downstream language-model inference cost. Existing token-reduction methods assess token utility through token-wise importance, query relevance, coverage, pairwise diversity, or subset-level objectives. Our key insight is to view visual token reduction through selected-span complementarity: instead of scoring a token in isolation or through pairwise relations, we assess how much of its feature is orthogonal to the span of the already retained subset. Based on this perspective, we propose Greedy Orthogonal Token Selection (GOTS), a training-free and query-agnostic method. At each step, GOTS selects the token with the largest residual energy orthogonal to the current retained span. This rule exactly maximizes the one-step augmented Gram determinant among candidate additions, giving each greedy step a precise local geometric guarantee for subset expansion. Across five high-resolution VLM backbones from the Qwen-VL and InternVL families and eleven diverse benchmarks, GOTS achieves higher average performance retention than the strongest evaluated baselines, and a controlled OCRBench study shows that it reduces model-side time-to-first-token after accounting for selection overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/newLLing/GOTS.

</details>


### [318] [Reality Monitoring in Large Language Models: Self-Knowledge That Transforms with Conversation Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23927)
*Saurabh Ranjan,Konstantina Sokratous,Brian Odegaard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两项实验和六个LLM，发现大型语言模型（LLMs）在对话中无法可靠区分自己生成的内容和用户输入的内容（即现实监控能力）。当记忆延迟增加时，模型对自生成内容的准确率从天花板水平骤降，出现内部与外部判断混淆、准确率与置信度解离等问题。这表明评估AI“知道什么”不够，还需追踪“知识来源”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLMs是否具备人类式的现实监控能力（区分自生成内容与外部输入），因为如果缺乏这种能力，模型会将自身错误当作用户提供的事实，加剧幻觉、妄想和虚构问题。

Method: 设计两项实验，在六种不同LLM上测试对话记忆结构对来源归因的影响，操控记忆负荷（最小延迟 vs. 情节延迟），并分析反馈信号对内部/外部判断及置信度的影响。

Result: 最小记忆需求下自生成内容的准确率达天花板水平；引入情节延迟后，准确率反转，外部项目优势变得脆弱；部分模型内部与外部判断发生互换，另一些模型准确率提升但置信度与正确性解离。活跃参数数量（而非总参数）是关键因素。

Conclusion: LLMs缺乏可靠的现实监控能力，现有基准无法检测此类缺陷。随着AI承担自主多轮交互角色，除了评估模型“知道什么”，追踪“知识来源”同等重要。

Abstract: A conversational AI that cannot tell its own output from what a user said will treat its own mistakes as user-provided facts. In humans, this capacity is called reality monitoring, and its failures are linked to hallucinations, delusions, and confabulation, yet whether LLMs possess it remains untested. Here we show, across two experiments and six LLMs, that source attribution depends on how conversational memory is structured: ceiling accuracy for self-generated content under minimal memory demands reverses to a fragile external-item advantage once episodic delay removes that shortcut. Feedback exposes two failures: in some models, internal and external judgments swap; in others, accuracy improves while confidence decouples from correctness, dissociations invisible to existing benchmarks. Across models, this pattern implicates active, not aggregate, parameter count. This suggests that as AI systems take on autonomous, multi-turn roles, evaluating what they know is not enough: tracking where that knowledge came from may matter equally.

</details>


### [319] [MemTX: Transactional Belief Commit for Stateful Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23929)
*Xiaoyang Li,Yiqi Wang,Haohui Lu,Zhi Chen,Mo Li,Pingan Song,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemTX提出了一种事务性信念提交协议，将记忆写入视为需验证的事务而非即时事实，通过快照隔离、验证-提交流水线和级联修复机制，确保LLM多智能体协作中工具调用的安全性，在550万协议状态测试中零违规，并超越所有基线方法。


<details>
